Net: `macro_leverage_cap` a constant 1.0 — macro constrained nothing. **FLOW:** `exchange_flow` sums `raw["exchange_flows"]`, a list no configured source populates, so it is a **structural 0.0** and `flow_direction` (ADVISE weight 0.20) has been pinned at 0 for the life of the system; the one live component (tape whale detector, BTC +1.00/0.64) is one of the 12 deliberately zero-weighted agents (P228). **SENTIMENT:** L1 real and fusion-consumed; L3/Haiku dead (`headlines=0` — CryptoPanic 429 + CC quota 90/90); LunarCrush dead by the same no-producer bug; options PCR pinned at 1.0 (P218's 404s).
- **The sharpest finding — the one live signal of the three is miscalibrated.** `market_data["sentiment_zscore"]` is **not a z-score**: it is `(fg-50)/50*3`, a fixed linear rescale, because the feed asks for `limit=1`. Fusion derives the agent's confidence as `min(|z|/3,1)`, so a hardcoded scale sets the strength of a live ADVISE input. The same free endpoint serves **3116 daily rows back to 2018-02-01**. At today's F&G=31 the linear form says **z=-1.14 (confidently bearish, conf 0.38, emitted on all three assets every tick)** while the real trailing-365d distribution says **z=+0.25, 71st percentile** — the past year averaged ~27, so 31 is *above* average.
- **"Do we need new sources?" — NO, at zero added cost.** Macro (FRED, key held, unlimited), social (LunarCrush, key held) and **exchange netflow** were all already available: a live probe found `/api/exchange/balance/list` works on the **existing CoinGlass key** (BTC 21 exchanges/2.51M, ETH 20/12.07M, SOL **zero**). Two additions are free and keyless: **Deribit** (782 BTC / 654 ETH option instruments + DVOL) and alternative.me history. Deliberately NOT added: yfinance (a fragile scraper reaching worse data than the official API we already authenticate to), a replacement news feed (P228 records llm_sentiment sign-flipping between windows = noise; fix the billing before buying), Trading Economics. **The one purchase that might be justified — SOL exchange flow / options (CryptoQuant/Glassnode, ~$30-100/mo) — is explicitly deferred:** buying data for a signal that has not yet cleared P166 on the assets we CAN measure is the P269 mistake.
- **Shipped, all default-OFF (P141/P201 trio, absent from the live profile and test-pinned absent):** FRED+LunarCrush `fetch_if_stale()` wired into the existing per-tick parallel feed driver (the same block whose own comment records `[BUGFIX DATA-1] OnChainFeed.fetch() was never called` — found for onchain, never for these two); `feed_provenance()` so `source_status` is derived from whether a feed HOLDS data rather than from dict shape (the old `if adapter_context.get("macro")` was True unconditionally — the P2/P174 missing-vs-neutral collapse, which is *why* the emptiness stayed invisible); `deribit_feed.py`; `exchange_netflow_feed.py`; `fear_greed_history.py` (persisted, refuses below 60 samples); GCI indicators from FRED via `fred_macro_series.py` + `update_if_stale()`.
- **Three traps handled explicitly, each of which would have made things WORSE than the zero it replaced:** (a) **the sign trap** — the netflow producer convention (`onchain_feed`: positive=inflow) is the OPPOSITE of the main.py consumer's (`"outflow = bullish" → +1`); they have disagreed forever and it never surfaced only because the producer always returned exactly 0.0, so the new feed publishes its own unambiguous keys and the bridge **negates explicitly**, pinned by test; (b) **the ETF fabrication trap** — driving GCI while its yfinance-backed ETF tracker returns MOCK flows would inject invented fund flows into `macro_regime`, so that path now REFUSES (returns None) and the real ETF data stays in P270's `EtfFlowShadow`; (c) **the two-directional cap** — GCI's leverage-cap map both tightens (RISK_ON 0.7) and **LOOSENS** (RISK_OFF 1.2, CRISIS 1.5), so driving it is not a one-way safety fix.
- **Absences kept as absences** (a fabricated value here is indistinguishable from a measurement — the P2/P223 defect): SOL options + SOL DVOL (Deribit lists ZERO SOL options), SOL exchange netflow (CoinGlass returns ZERO SOL exchanges), GOLD (FRED's daily series is discontinued), ETF flows inside GCI. DXY is served by `DTWEXBGS` and **labelled a PROXY** (different basket, ~119 vs ~104) — used only through its scale-free z-score.
- **Pre-committed activation order** (§4 of the plan, fixed before any evidence exists so a later flip cannot be selection): options_use_deribit (near-zero risk — zero-weighted agent) → macro_gci_live (2wk stability) → sentiment_zscore_mode (30d, both forms logged, P166 comparison) → exchange_netflow_to_flow_agent (30d + P166; largest change, weight 0.20) → dvol_to_market_data **last** (it arms `EMERGENCY_FLAT` at z≥5, a path that has never once executed, P265d).
- Baselines hand-bumped with exact attribution, never `--update` (P171): tryexcept 682→691 (+9, per-guard attribution in the note); freshness BLIND 228→241 / total 290→303 / blind-only 148→156 (**exactly** the 8 new keys + 5 re-writes inside the default-OFF enforce branches). Six deliberate value-coercion refusals got `noqa: silent-swallow` + rationale at source rather than inflating the swallow counters.
- **Recorded, NOT claimed:** no new edge is demonstrated — this makes signals exist and be honest; `data_health` may move in EITHER direction now that MACRO can honestly report `defaults_only` (it dampens quant_confidence ×0.85/×0.6), which is correct but is a behaviour change; and "the linear z-score is miscalibrated" does **not** imply the historical one is profitable — that is what criterion 3 exists to settle.
- **[P293b — same day, operator: "fix all"] The two items I called operator-only were each hiding a real code defect, and one of my diagnoses was simply WRONG.**
  - **CryptoPanic: I claimed "~6 req/day against a 3000/mo plan, so the 429 cannot be volume — it is billing/tier". Probing from the server returned the API's own verdict: `429 {"status":"api_error","info":"API monthly quota exceeded - Upgrade your API plan"}`.** So it IS a plan matter — but two real bugs sat on top. (1) **A monthly quota was being retried like a transient rate limit:** CryptoPanic sends NO `Retry-After`, so the handler took its 900s default and re-asked **every 15 minutes for the rest of the month** (~2,900 pointless requests, each logging a warning that reads like a transient fault — the P202 shape). Now the 429 BODY is parsed; a monthly-quota message backs off to the 1st of next month UTC and says once that this is a BUDGET state. An unrecognised 429 still takes the short backoff — fail direction is "retry sooner than necessary", never "go dark for weeks by mistake". (2) **A Cloudflare client-signature hazard:** from the server the default stdlib UA returns **`HTTP 403 error code: 1010`** (Cloudflare's banned-client code) — a DIFFERENT failure from a rate limit that would keep a feed dark even with quota available; `create_session()` now sets a User-Agent for every feed.
  - **CryptoCompare: the budget was not the problem, the RESTARTS were.** The 90/90 pool was exhausted by **Aug 10, nine days into the month**, against a designed demand of ~3 calls/day. Cause: **both feeds kept their throttle clock in RAM.** `cc_onchain`'s check is `now - self._last_fetch_time < MIN_FETCH_INTERVAL and self._data` and BOTH halves reset on restart; `cc_news` cached in memory only. **P253d had persisted the BACKOFF one line below — leaving the throttle in memory, directly under its own comment that "a limiter that re-arms on restart is not a limiter".** Measured **14 process starts** across the retained logs. Both feeds now persist the throttle clock AND the payload (restoring only the clock would leave `and self._data` False and the persistence decorative — the exact trap P253d named), with mock rows never persisted (a restored mock would survive as if it were a reading, P2). **No budget change: raising `HMATS_CC_MONTHLY_BUDGET` would have masked a restart bug with a bigger allowance against a 100/month cap that cannot be upgraded.**
  - **The flow sign disagreement FIXED AT SOURCE, not bridged.** P293 negated inside the new bridge; the honest fix is the consumer — `flow_direction` mapped POSITIVE (= inflow, the producer's convention) to **+1 bullish**, the inverse of its own comment. Corrected in main.py so producer and consumer share ONE convention and the bridge passes straight through. Behaviour-neutral today (the key is always 0.0, sign-invariant) and correct the moment it carries a number — which is precisely the right time to fix a sign, before any data can be misread by it.
  - **GCI ETF: real flows instead of the refusal.** Reuses P270's `EtfFlowShadow` reader (CoinGlass v4, incl. its in-progress-day guard) rather than a second implementation of the same endpoint (P172). ONE aggregate record **per asset** — the caller SUMS the list, so one per *ticker* would multiply the real flow by the ticker count. Flow older than 4 days reads as ABSENT, not current. First live read: **BTC −$56.2M, ETH flat.** **Caught mid-build:** `_calculate_streak` derives the asset from the ticker and buckets ANY non-BTC ticker as ETH, so the synthetic aggregate ticker would have silently moved ETH's streak — extracted `_calculate_streak_for_asset` (one implementation, two entry points) and pinned the mis-bucketing as the reason it exists.
  - **A falsification probe caught a VACUOUS pin of my own:** `test_persist_runs_on_success_not_only_on_429` asserted `src.count("_persist_backoff()") >= 2` and stayed GREEN when the success-path call was deleted, because the two rate-limit call sites already satisfied the count. **A count is not a location** — rewritten to assert the call sits inside the SUCCESS block, then re-probed red (P174/P238). A second anchor was non-unique (`if not YFINANCE_AVAILABLE:` matched an earlier site and swallowed unrelated code) and was scoped to the function.
  - Tests: `tests/test_p293_data_coverage.py` 59 → **82**; 13 falsification probes red-then-restored. Baseline tryexcept 691→694 hand-bumped with per-guard attribution.
- **[P293c — operator: "cheaper option for cryptopanic / helius_api_key unable to set / promote whale"] Two of the three asks dissolved on inspection, and BOTH dissolutions were my earlier errors.**
  - **CHEAPER CRYPTOPANIC = free.** No cheaper VENDOR is worth buying, because a free one publishes the only thing consumed: `SentimentLLMAgent` sends **headline TEXT** to Haiku and derives sentiment itself — it does not use CryptoPanic's vote tallies for direction, so the expensive half (curated metadata) is not what the tradeable signal rests on. Public RSS is keyless/unquota'd and probed **94 headlines/poll** (cointelegraph 30, decrypt 34, theblock 20, bitcoinmagazine 10; CoinDesk 308-redirects and is OMITTED — an unverified redirect target is not a source). New `data_mgmt/feeds/rss_news_feed.py` wired as a THIRD blend after CryptoPanic and CC News, deliberately last and additive (can only add what the curated sources missed), with the SAME 4h window + dedup so stale copy cannot satisfy the `_c3_live` starvation gate. **Verified end-to-end with NO API KEYS: BTC 5 fresh headlines, ETH 1, where both were 0.** Does NOT fabricate `panic_score`/`news_velocity`/`narrative_intensity` — RSS has no vote data (test-pinned absent). Relevance matching is **word-bounded**: a naive `"sol" in title` matches solution/sold/console/solar and would flood SOL with unrelated news (pinned with a 5-trap test).
  - **HELIUS: I WAS WRONG — nothing to set, nothing to buy.** I listed it as an operator action because the key was absent from `.env`; that was inference, not verification. `SOLANA_RPC_URL` **is already set** on the server to the public endpoint, `solana_onchain.py` defaults to `https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com` (keyless, answered on probe), and the feed is **live and healthy** — TPS ~4,072, slot times, Jito tip percentiles, refreshing every ~2 min. `HELIUS_API_KEY` is referenced only by an UNSELECTED source enum in `onchain_feed.py`. That `onchain_sol` shows a flat direction is a separate question about what a congestion/TPS feed can say about DIRECTION — not a missing credential.
  - **WHALE PROMOTION: refused as specified, with two measured reasons.** (1) **Its own instrument says HOLD:** `agent_ic_review --window-days 60` → 4h IC +0.053 t=1.15 edge 3.8bps (required 0.168); 16h IC +0.024 **t=0.26** edge 3.2bps (required 0.092). Positive but statistically indistinguishable from zero and economically below cost. (2) **Adding an ADVISE weight is a measured NO-OP on live orders:** the weight enters as `contribution = w × confidence × alignment`, scaling `base_exposure` — a MAGNITUDE, never a direction — and that value is **overwritten unconditionally at `integration_v36.py:1678`** (`intent.target_exposure = tranche_decision.target_exposure`, "Bug #44"), and even surviving that, the sleeve sizes by SIGN (`target_for(asset, dir)`) and discards magnitude. Two independent mechanisms kill it, confirming P231's claim still holds. **Shipping the weight would have looked like a promotion and changed nothing — the P177 shape — so it was NOT shipped.** The real levers are recorded in the plan §5d: (A) entry filter on the P236 `ma_filter` pattern (best fit for a weak-but-positive signal — a veto is more defensible than a driver), (B) a direction seat on the P285 `mlp` pattern (not justified at t=0.26), (C) fixing Bug #44 (large refactor that re-arms the whole dead pre-1678 exposure stack).
  - Tests 82 → **92**; 3 more falsification probes red-then-restored (one needed a raw-string anchor before it would bite). Two deliberate date-coercion refusals `noqa`'d at source; zero baseline movement.
- **[P293d — operator: "end-to-end check if anything besides whale should be promoted / build A,B,C / audit data usage, sentiment, and whether signals play downstream"] The structural answer: 22 of 26 agents CANNOT reach a live order through fusion, and it has nothing to do with their weights.**
  - **THE ARCHITECTURAL FINDING (traced in code, the answer to "do the signals actually play downstream"): only DIRECTION survives fusion, and only DECIDE agents set it.** `LAYER 3` is the sole place `result.direction` is assigned (`result.direction = decider_signal.direction`); **every other layer — CONFIRM (regime/two_stage/structure), HTF, PARTIAL CONSENSUS, ADVISE (18 agents), CAP (macro) — modifies `base_exposure` only** (pinned by test: `result.direction` must not appear in the CONFIRM or ADVISE blocks). That exposure is then severed TWICE: `intent.target_exposure` is overwritten at `integration_v36.py:1678` by the tranche (Bug #44 — itself a REAL fix that stopped an unchecked 80% fusion exposure reaching the risk governor, so it is deliberately NOT reverted), and the sleeve sizes by SIGN, discarding magnitude. `coinbase_use_gated_intent=True`, so the intent DOES drive the sleeve — but the live chain is `trend_decision_layer → quant slot → LAYER 3 → direction → gated intent → sleeve sign`, and everything below LAYER 3 is instrumentation. **This is why an ADVISE weight is a no-op and why option A had to act on the sleeve DRIVER.**
  - **PROMOTION SWEEP — nothing else is ready.** `agent_ic_review --window-days 90`, all 9 measurable agents: llm_sentiment +0.040(t=1.12)/+0.048(0.67), sentiment +0.001/+0.049, whale +0.040(0.98)/+0.011(0.14), model_alpha +0.005/+0.030, micro +0.007/+0.202(n=35), **quant — the DECIDER — +0.007/−0.046**, drl −0.005/−0.059, funding −0.014/−0.138, kraken_quant INSUFFICIENT(n=23). Required IC 0.159(4h)/0.085(16h); **every agent is an order of magnitude short and every |t| < 1.2.** Nothing is being unfairly held back. Worth naming without over-reading: the agent driving the book has the second-worst 16h IC while several zero-weighted agents are mildly positive — at |t|<1.2 that is noise-vs-noise, not an argument for a swap, but it is why option B exists as a config flip.
  - **A — WHALE ENTRY FILTER (built, default OFF).** `sleeve_agent_filter_decision` extracted from `sleeve_ma_filter_decision`, which now DELEGATES to it (P172 one implementation two entry points; parity pinned across the full 125-case truth table so the reason strings and behaviour are unchanged for P236). New `whale_filter` ledger prefix registered at BOTH scorer sites — **inserted BEFORE `ma_filter` because the P236 guard's regex pins ma_filter at the END of the CLI default; appending would have broken another workstream's guard, and weakening a guard to admit a change is never the fix (the P248 lesson, hit again).** Invariants pinned: a silent agent fails OPEN (a dead agent must not become a standing entry veto, P208), exits are never filtered, an aligned HELD position is never force-exited (an entry filter must not become an exit engine), a flip is demoted to flat not reversed.
  - **B — WHALE DIRECTION SEAT (built, default OFF).** Same quant-slot injection as the trend/regimebook/mlp seats, placed LAST so precedence is deterministic and stated rather than accidental. Asserts the SAME 30bps×|dir| alpha bar (a seat swap changes the DIRECTION source, never the bar — P231/P237 govern that constant). **The ±0.3 deadband is now single-sourced in `whale_direction_from_pressure`** because the seat runs BEFORE the bridge writes the per-asset stash, and a second copy of a threshold is how two consumers of "the whale signal" start disagreeing. Absent producer → seat NOT taken (absence is not flat, P2). **Explicitly not justified on evidence (60d 16h t=0.26) — built so promotion is a config flip WHEN evidence supports it, not a code change under time pressure.**
  - **C — FUSION CONVICTION TO SLEEVE SIZING (built, default OFF).** The sleeve's own docstring named the gap: *"partial sizes would need a conviction-magnitude input the ±1-era driver never had"* — that is exactly what C supplies. `FusionResult.fusion_conviction` records final/pre-modulation exposure as one ratio (everything layers 4-6 did), stamped onto a DECLARED `TradeIntentV36` field (P239 — not a dynamic attribute), and passed to `target_for(..., conviction)`. **Reconnects the exposure channel WITHOUT reverting Bug #44.** Fail directions: unusable conviction (NaN/inf/negative) leaves size UNCHANGED, never 0 — a fabricated 0 would read as "every advisor said flat", a very different claim from "the ratio was unreadable" (P2); values >1.0 clamped (doubt, never leverage); sign never changed. **HONEST LIMIT, documented and pinned: at 1-3 contracts this mostly rounds to the same integer — BTC trades 1ct, so any conviction >0.5 rounds back to 1 — so the channel is near-binary at current equity and becomes meaningful only as the book grows.** Recorded rather than oversold.
  - **SENTIMENT AUDIT — the pipeline is healthy; the INTERPRETATION is three stacked assertions, and two of them contradict each other.** `main.py:7338` maps F&G by MOMENTUM (`(fg-50)/50*3` → fear = bearish) and that is what reaches fusion (F&G=31 → z=−1.14 → `sentiment_direction=−1.0`, conf 0.38, seen live on all three assets). But `signals/deterministic_sentiment.py` — the module CLAUDE.md lists as "Sentiment L1 (F&G) ACTIVE" — implements the CONTRARIAN reading (greed −0.6, extreme fear 0.0 "don't chase shorts"), and its output is logged but never becomes `sentiment_direction`. **They disagree in sign whenever the market is greedy.** Also measured: **the contrarian engine can never emit a bullish value** (every branch ≤ 0 — structurally short-biased, so "sentiment turned bullish" is not an output it can produce). So the live signal is a hardcoded linear rescale (not a z-score, P293), read as momentum (not contrarian), crossing a fixed |z|>1.0 band (≈ F&G outside 33-67) — three asserted choices stacked, and the 90d IC (+0.001/+0.049, both insignificant) cannot distinguish right from wrong. **Recommended, NOT built: add a contrarian variant to the ledger that already logs both z-score forms — three claims, one exam, zero live risk.**
  - Tests: `tests/test_p293d_whale_options.py` (45); 6 falsification probes. **TWO were vacuous and were rewritten:** the magnitude-clamp pin survived removing one of two redundant clamps (rewritten to assert the OUTCOME on both implementations), and the parity pin fed only VALID convictions so it never reached the refusal branch where the two could most easily disagree (a probe making the sleeve return 0 on NaN left it green). A third failure was my own test stripping comments and then searching for comment markers. Baselines hand-bumped with attribution: tryexcept 694→695, freshness TIMESTAMPED 19→22 (total_writes unchanged — the seat REWRITES existing keys, exactly like the seats it mirrors).
- **[P293e/f/g — operator: "build the contrarian variant / if everything is off and never promoted why bother / validate we fetch APIs efficiently, research it"] The "why bother" question measured, and the answer is a defect nobody had converted into the right units.**
  - **e — THE SENTIMENT EXAM (built, observation-only).** `defense/sentiment_variant_shadow.py` records three competing F&G readings per tick to `sentvariant_{ASSET}.jsonl`, judged by the SAME P166 gate: `sent_momentum_linear` (the live rule), `sent_momentum_hist` (same rule on the real historical z), `sent_contrarian` (the deterministic engine's own asymmetric mapping). Verified they genuinely disagree — **at F&G=80 momentum says +1 BULLISH and contrarian says −1 BEARISH.** The contrarian variant is deliberately NOT `-momentum`: a pure negation has an IC exactly the negative and would add zero information; the recorded claim is the engine's ASYMMETRIC mapping (greed penalised hard, extreme fear → **neutral, not bullish**), pinned by a test that fails if it ever becomes a mirror image. Prefix registered at both scorer sites with `ma_filter` still last (the P236 end-anchor, P248 lesson).
  - **g — WHY NOTHING PROMOTES: the 30-day clock CANNOT FIRE at 16h.** Measured: of 41 config gates, 15 are decided in the live profile and **11 are ON** — so "everything is off" is half wrong. But every promoted item is EXECUTION / COST / RISK (maker-first, reprice, venue-aware fees/funding/spreads/hold, routing, gated intent, fast-risk, flip-persistence, trend enforce), **never alpha** — because their evidence is ARITHMETIC (a maker fill costs 0 vs 3bps; no 30-day window needed). The alpha side is where the defect is. The gate needs `|t| = IC·sqrt(n_eff−1) ≥ 2` with `n_eff = n/h`, so at 6 bars/day: **a 30-day window can only certify IC ≥ 0.302 at 16h, while the ECONOMIC bar asks ~0.13 — the statistical bar is ~2.3× stricter, and an economically-adequate candidate needs ~330 DAYS.** The best live IC ever observed (0.048) would need ~3 years. **So the ~14 candidates on 30-day clocks against 16h horizons are on clocks that structurally cannot fire — a CHECK THAT CANNOT PASS, the exact inverse of P174's check-that-cannot-fail, and a defect by the same reasoning.** It hid because the gate reported its requirement in SAMPLES (`n_required~=1980`) and nobody converted it: the blocker line now reads `= ~330d of 4H bars vs ~30d held`. At 4h the arithmetic is fine (economic bar binds, ~27 days suffices) — the defect is horizon-specific, which is exactly why the gate must state it per horizon. **Recommendation recorded: stop adding alpha shadows until the clock question is settled** (lengthen to 6-12 months, or judge those claims at 4h, or pool assets to raise n) — a fourteenth costs maintenance and cannot produce a verdict sooner than the thirteen already running.
  - **f — API EFFICIENCY, researched then fixed.** Probed every dependency's response headers: **only RSS exposes validators** (`last-modified` + `s-maxage=300`); alternative.me, CoinGlass and Deribit return no ETag, no Last-Modified (Deribit says `no-store`) and **no rate-limit headers at all** — which is why client-side quota accounting (P220) is the only way to know where we stand. So conditional requests are available on exactly one source, and TTL-matched-to-update-cadence is the lever everywhere else. Waste found and fixed: **CoinGlass (PAID) had NO throttle on `fetch()` while the tick calls it once per ASSET and `_fetch_real` already loops all three symbols internally across 3 endpoint families — ~3× the requests needed**; **F&G is a DAILY value whose outer staleness check reads the DATA timestamp (so it is stale on every asset) and was refetched per asset**. Both now use `fetch_if_stale()`. RSS got a real conditional GET — **verified 4× `304` on the second fetch with the corpus intact** (a 304 REUSES that source's items; returning `[]` would read as "this source went silent"). `cache_age_seconds()` now has ONE definition instead of a sixth hand-rolled copy, handling datetime and float-epoch stamps with the P40/P97 naive/aware defence. Recorded as already-correct so nobody "fixes" it again: CC News serves all three assets in one request (P219), the CC budget is account-wide and persisted (P220), and every throttle survives restart (P293b); the remaining per-symbol loops are INSIDE one throttled fetch and are inherent — those APIs have no multi-symbol endpoint.
  - Tests: `tests/test_p293e_sentiment_and_efficiency.py` (28); 6 falsification probes all red. One test bug caught: selecting the feed class by NAME picked dataclasses (`FREDObservation`) — rewritten to select by CAPABILITY (the class that owns `fetch`), which also makes it catch any future feed that gains `fetch()` without a throttle. Zero baseline movement (one coercion refusal `noqa`'d at source).
  - **Mitigation pattern:** a promotion gate states its requirement in whatever units its arithmetic produces, and if nobody converts them the requirement is invisible — `n_required~=1980` and "~330 days" are the same fact, but only one of them tells you the 30-day clock beside it is theatre. Report thresholds in the units of the DECISION (calendar time), not of the computation (samples). And before assuming a caching problem, probe for `ETag`/`Last-Modified`: the textbook fix is unavailable on most JSON APIs, which changes the answer from "conditional requests" to "TTL matched to the data's update cadence, plus one fetch serving all assets".
- **[P293h/i — operator: "do we really need the tripwire, the 14 shadow clocks, and the 30-day contrarian exam"] Two of the three were answerable immediately, and the question surfaced a live-money reporting bug plus a coupling nobody had noticed.**
  - **h — A DEPOSIT WAS BEING REPORTED AS PROFIT (fixed).** `log_pnl_point` computed `pnl = eq - start_equity`, so the 2026-08-16 transfer of **$7,074** was booked as gain: the sleeve ledger read **+$6,850 (+171%)** while actual trading PnL over the same 65 days was **−$225 (−6.3%)** — a **177-percentage-point misreport in the flattering direction**, on the one number that answers "is this making money". P274/P287 documented the WITHDRAWAL direction (reads as drawdown against the sticky halt anchor) and prescribed a manual re-anchor; the DEPOSIT direction was never handled and the manual step was not performed. Now: bounded flow detection (a step exceeding max(≈$200, 0.5×position notional) cannot be PnL on a few nano contracts), excluded from PnL, **percentage rebased on invested capital** (start + net flows) so the ratio stays comparable, `external_flow_usd` recorded on every row so the adjustment is auditable, and the accumulator **persisted** (without it a restart re-books the transfer as profit — the P154 class). Fail direction deliberate: an ambiguous step stays classified as PnL — the honest-but-noisy reading — never a silent adjustment that could hide a real loss.
  - **i — THE CONTRARIAN EXAM WAS SUPERSEDED BEFORE IT COLLECTED A RECORD.** A 30-day forward exam was the wrong instrument for a question 6 years of data already answers: the F&G history is 3,116 days and price history is on disk. Backtested all three readings on ~2,175 days × 3 assets — **MOMENTUM wins every cell (BTC/ETH/SOL 7d IC +0.056/+0.088/+0.126) and CONTRARIAN loses every cell by construction** (it is the exact negation). So the LIVE `main.py:7338` momentum reading is correct and `deterministic_sentiment.py`'s contrarian mapping is the one that must never be promoted. Honest caveat recorded: alternative.me builds F&G partly FROM price (volatility 25% + momentum/volume 25%), so this settles WHICH INTERPRETATION to use, not that F&G is independent alpha.
  - **THE COUPLING, which changes what "fire the tripwire" means.** `[SENT-SWITCH]` (`market_data_pipeline.py:1189`) is a THIRD F&G interpretation — fear<25 boosts mean_revert, greed>75 boosts momentum — feeding the Best-of-N **strategy selector**, i.e. `quant_direction`, the DECIDE agent. It fires on **30.8% of days** historically (**171 extreme-fear days in the last 365, zero extreme-greed**), and is dormant today ONLY because `trend_following_mode: enforce` overwrites `quant_direction` after the pipeline computes it. **So removing the trend injection does NOT take the book flat — it hands the wheel to Best-of-N, whose weights are modulated by a never-validated rule, in a regime where it fires ~47% of days.** Anyone firing the tripwire must decide what takes over FIRST.
  - **Mitigation pattern:** before scheduling a forward exam, check whether the question is answerable from history you already hold — a 30-day clock is the most expensive way to answer something a 30-second backtest settles, and here the historical data (3,116 days) had just been added by the same batch that proposed the wait. And when a decision "turns something off", trace what becomes the decider in its absence: an override can be the only thing keeping an unvalidated path dormant.
- **[P293j — ARMED 2026-08-17, operator: "the key issue is that we build the system that does no trade… let the whale turn on"] The no-trade cause measured, the tripwire DECIDED (delegated), and the whale seat armed — because whale's BINARY encoding is what clears the alpha gate that trend's fractional signal cannot.**
  - **THE NO-TRADE CAUSE, measured (the CLAUDE.md Trade-Frequency rule applied, belatedly):** ~7 order events across both retained logs; every tick summary reads `dir=+0.00` on all three assets and the gate logs `Alpha 10bps` against thresholds of 19-60bps. **The binding constraint is SIGNAL STRENGTH, not the tripwire:** trend emits |dir|≈0.33 → `40 × 0.33 × 0.75 = 10bps`, below every threshold, so the book sits flat. The P291b venue-true thresholds (BTC 19.1 / ETH 26.7 / SOL 29.0) are all still above 10bps, and the asserted-alpha ceiling at full conviction is 30bps — so ONLY a near-full-conviction signal can trade at all.
  - **WHY WHALE FIXES IT, and the honest caveat:** whale emits **±1.0 BINARY**, so it asserts the full 30bps whenever it speaks — clearing all three thresholds. Measured firing rate over 60d of attribution: **BTC 54% / ETH 43% / SOL 12%** of ticks directional, and whale speaks when trend cannot on **30% / 25% / 11%**. **Whale clears the gate because it is BINARY, not because it is better evidenced** — its live IC is +0.040 (4h) / +0.011 (16h), statistically indistinguishable from zero. It is however better than the INCUMBENT quant's **negative −0.046** at 16h, so the swap is defensible on relative grounds. Armed by explicit operator instruction, deliberately trading "no trades at all" for "trades on a weak but positive signal"; flip-persistence (2 ticks), the alpha gate, caps, stops and halts all still bind.
  - **THE BUG THAT WOULD HAVE SHIPPED A FLATTENER (caught before arming):** the seat was modelled on the P285 mlp seat, which emits CONTINUOUSLY — whale does not. As written, a whale inside its ±0.3 deadband would have **seated 0.0 and forced the book flat on 46/57/88% of ticks**, which is both a churn engine and the P2 error (absence read as a position), and would have destroyed the fallback that drives those ticks. Fixed: a silent whale does NOT take the seat; the incumbent stands. **Copying a seat pattern without checking the new signal's SPARSITY is the trap.**
  - **THE TRIPWIRE DECISION (delegated by the operator, made here): DO NOT FIRE IT NOW.** The evidence for trend having no edge is strong (16h IC −0.046, calibrator 2/2 GATE-CLOSED on all three, −$225 realized over 65d). **But firing it does not take the book flat — it hands the wheel to Best-of-N, whose strategy weights are modulated by `[SENT-SWITCH]`, an unvalidated F&G rule that fires on ~47% of days in the current regime (P293i).** Removing a measured-weak signal into an unmeasured one is worse, not better. With whale seated, trend's practical role is already near-nil (it is blocked at 10bps on most ticks), so the tripwire's intent is satisfied in effect without activating the unvalidated path. **Re-scoped: at the 09-01 date the comparison should be whale-seat forward results vs trend, not a blind removal into Best-of-N.**
  - **Process note worth keeping:** the first config edit rewrote the whole `live_high_risk.json` via `json.dumps(indent=2)` — 160 insertions, and `0.10` silently became `0.1`, which tripped the authority scanner's drift check. Reverted and re-applied as a **3-line surgical insert**. A config rewrite is never a formatting-neutral operation; edit the text, not the parsed object.
### P295. [INSPECTED + BUILT 2026-08-18, operator: "inspect if our regimebook and trend setup right, and build the seat controller"] The trend signal is a 3-vote quantization that can only assert 10bps or 30bps — so it trades ONLY on unanimity — and the seat controller replaces the tripwire's kill-only shape
- **TREND INSPECTION — the precise mechanism of "the system does no trade".** `TrendFollowingStrategy.compute` is a VOTE: `sig += 1 if mom>0 else -1` over `lookbacks_days=(10,20,40)`, then `sig /= 3`. So **`sig` is quantized to {±1/3, ±1} — nothing in between.** Through the live alpha assertion (`40 × |sig| × 0.75`) that is **10bps (2-of-3 vote) or 30bps (unanimous)**, against live thresholds of 19.1/26.7/29.0. **A 2-of-3 majority is therefore untradeable BY CONSTRUCTION, and trend trades only when all three lookbacks agree.** Not a bug — but it is the exact reason the tick summaries read `dir=+0.00` with `Alpha 10bps` while the signal is never actually flat.
- **And a P174-class dead control found beside it: `min_abs_signal = 0.30` can NEVER fire.** It zeroes `|sig| < 0.30`, but the smallest reachable `|sig|` is 1/3 = 0.3333. The "weak trend → flat" branch is unreachable, so **trend always has an opinion**; it is the GATE, not the strategy, that produces the flat book. Pinned, including a guard that a floor rising above 1/3 would be a live behaviour change rather than a tidy-up.
- **REGIMEBOOK INSPECTION — one candidate is fine, one is correct-but-idle, one is structurally dead.** BTC `v1_full` works, but over 71 ticks **every in-market tick came from its FUNDING legs** (funding_short 14, funding_contrarian_short 6, flat 51) and **zero from its trend leg** — and P262 records the BTC funding legs as the **UNCERTIFIED** slice of that book, so seating BTC regimebook today would trade precisely the part that was never certified. ETH `v1_full` is `trend_flat` on 71/71 — **correct, not broken**: it is a trend-only book (P247) and the regime window held no bull (peace 37 / bear 34). SOL is `v1_degraded_no_bear_leg` on 71/71 because **P250 DELETED `configs/regimebook/SOL_bear_ridge.json`** (it was the leak artifact) and `configs/regimebook/` does not exist at all — so SOL's book is permanently inert and must be treated as UNAVAILABLE, never as "a candidate that says flat".
- **SEAT CONTROLLER BUILT** (`analytics/seat/seat_controller.py` + `scripts/seat_check.py`, 30 tests, 6 falsification probes all red). Replaces the tripwire's shape — one variable, one threshold, one direction, whose entire action space is "trade less" and therefore cannot serve a goal of trading. Design rules, each with its reason: **multi-variable** (the literature's requirement); **weakest-horizon scoring** `min(ic_4h, ic_16h)` so a candidate must be non-negative on BOTH (this system's signals repeatedly look fine at 4h and go negative at 16h — P198, P293k); **hysteresis** (a challenger must clear the incumbent by 0.02 IC, else the seat thrashes on noise — the flip-persistence argument, and Gârleanu-Pedersen's partial-adjustment result); **significance floor** (|t| ≥ 1.0 on the decisive horizon — every IC here sits inside noise, so without it the controller chases random ordering weekly); **UNAVAILABLE ≠ FLAT** (the SOL case — scoring a broken candidate as a flat opinion lets it win by default, P2); and **it never edits config** (P141 — prints the exact edit, exits 3). `flat` is an implicit candidate with score 0.0, so **the tripwire's verdict is subsumed as one outcome reached by comparison** rather than by a single-variable threshold.
- **It independently reproduces the P293j decision on the real 90d numbers:** whale +0.0110 is the only candidate positive on both horizons; trend scores −0.046; regimebook is unscoreable. Winner: whale, HOLD. Each recommendation carries its winner's structural caveat in the OUTPUT (whale clears the gate because it is BINARY, not because it is better evidenced), so the caveat cannot be lost by living only in a doc.
- **[P295b — HOTFIX, same day, caught by reading the live log after deploy] The P295 wiring passed `min_abs_signal` to the singleton FACTORY, which did not accept it.** Every tick raised `TypeError: get_trend_decision_layer() got an unexpected keyword argument`, main.py's handler logged `[TREND-LAYER] process skip`, and **the trend layer produced NOTHING at all** — inert in the opposite direction from intended. It failed SAFE (no signal rather than a wrong one, and the whale seat still held the slot) but the control was not doing what the commit said. **The P295 test pinned the CALL SITE as a source string, which proves the argument is PASSED and says nothing about whether the callee accepts it — P234 verbatim, committed by the author quoting P234 two paragraphs earlier.** Fixed on the factory (and applied to an already-built singleton, or a config change would reach only a cold process); now covered by a test that CALLS the factory, plus a signature check that every kwarg main.py passes exists on it. **Mitigation pattern: verifying a deploy means reading the live log for the thing you changed, not checking the container came up healthy — this was invisible in `docker ps`, in the test suite, and in the CI gate, and visible in one grep of the first tick.**
- **[P295c — the tool could not run where its evidence lives.]** `scripts/` is excluded from the engine image by `.dockerignore` (the P141 allowlist: `scripts/` also holds `launch_live.py`/`coinbase_test_order.py`/`coinbase_flatten.py`, and order-placing code must not be baked into the live trading container). So `docker exec ... scripts/seat_check.py` failed with "No such file or directory" — a weekly decision tool shipped somewhere it cannot work, the P213 shape. Added to **BOTH** halves (Dockerfile COPY **and** a `.dockerignore` negation), which is the P192 rule: naming a file in the build recipe is not putting it in the build context, and one half alone makes the image unbuildable. The blanket exclusion stays. Now scheduled Mondays **06:40 UTC** (after all five existing evidence jobs), via a wrapper that resolves the NEWEST timestamped report at run time and **exits 2 when no report exists** — "no report" must never read as "flat wins".
- **[P295d — the first live run recommended vacating every seat on n=0 evidence. Two defects, and the second is the one that matters.]** (1) **Parser shape:** `agent_ic_review` emits `agents.<name>.horizons.<h>`; the reader read `agents.<name>.<h>`, so every agent parsed to `{}`, every candidate fell below the evidence floor, and the verdict was computed from nothing — **registered-but-unreadable, P264 verbatim**, and caught only because the tool was finally run against real producer output. (2) **The aggregate hole:** `decide_seat` asked "is any candidate positive?" without first asking "is any candidate SCOREABLE?", so with zero scoreable candidates **flat won by default** — the module's own rule 5 forbids exactly this one level down (UNAVAILABLE ≠ flat, so a broken candidate cannot win by default), and **the set-level version of that per-item rule is a different code path, which mine did not obey.** Now a distinct `refused` outcome: incumbent holds, no config edit printed, exit 2. **Flat may only win by COMPARISON** — at least one candidate measured and found non-positive.
- **[P295d — what the real evidence says, now that it can be read]** (30d, 2026-08-17 report): `quant` (the seat's own series) IC 4h **−0.0326** (t −0.87, n=713) / 16h **−0.086** (t −1.14, n=704) → score −0.086; `whale` 4h **−0.0698** (t −1.05, n=226) / 16h **−0.0651** (t −0.48, n=225) → score −0.070. Both scoreable, both negative on both horizons ⇒ **flat wins by comparison** (exit 3, a real verdict). **Two caveats travel with it and must not be dropped: the whale series over that window measures whale as an ADVISE agent, not as the seat it took on 2026-08-17, and quant's series is pre-P295 trend (the 10bps votes the gate vetoed).** Neither is evidence about the configuration now running — **the first honest read of the CURRENT book is the 2026-08-24 report.** The recommendation was deliberately NOT applied (P141).
- **Mitigation pattern:** a decision tool's first run against **real producer output** is part of shipping it, not a follow-up — this one was verdict-complete, fully tested, and wrong in the direction that recommends flattening a live book. And when a module states a rule about one entity, check the AGGREGATE obeys it too.
- **Mitigation pattern:** when a signal "never trades", inspect its RANGE before its logic — a vote over N lookbacks is quantized to N+1 values, and if the gate threshold falls between two of them the strategy is not weak, it is *unreachable*. And a floor constant (`min_abs_signal`) is only meaningful if the guarded quantity can actually land inside it; check the reachable set, not the comparison.
- **Mitigation pattern:** when several consumers of one datum all read neutral values, do not go looking for N bugs — check whether the datum has a PRODUCER at all. `grep` for `.fetch(`/`.start(` on the feed, not for readers of it. Three independent macro paths here died of the identical cause, and each had been "verified" by someone reading a consumer. And any accessor named `get_*` that returns a populated dict on an empty cache makes absence unfalsifiable: give it a provenance sibling, or the status flag is a constant wearing a measurement's name. **[P293b addendum]** Two more, both earned the hard way: (a) **an API's own error body outranks your arithmetic about its quota** — I reasoned from request-rate to "this cannot be a limit" and the endpoint said the opposite in plain text, so probe the vendor before writing off a whole class of cause; and (b) **a rate limiter is only as durable as the clock it reads** — persisting a backoff while leaving the throttle timestamp in RAM protects the rare case and leaves the common one (restart) uncovered, which is how a monthly budget disappears in nine days.

### P292. [BUILT 2026-08-17] XRP routing prerequisites completed — the September flip is now a config change with no code change, and the proposed sizing was wrong by an amount the net cap would have silently enforced
Completing what P291-C left: `exchange/symbol_mapping.py` gained the five breadth `coinbase/perp` entries matching the probed pids exactly (XRP→`XPP-20DEC30-CDE`, ADA→`ADP`, LTC→`LCP`, DOGE→`DOP`, BNB→`BNB-20DEC30-CDE`). P265h verified rather than assumed: its guard is `perp_pids - set(fallback_tables)`, one-directional, so P291-C's fallback-tables-first ordering makes this edit satisfy it (the reverse order breaks it). Spot maps deliberately untouched — no probe covered spot, and an unverified entry is the P265h fabricated-unit hazard; the P133/P135/P137 SOL/USDT asymmetry stays pinned.
- **The sizing correction (the finding worth keeping):** P291-C proposed XRP at fraction 0.10 (2ct). With the majors at 0.15 each the theoretical max net is 45%, so XRP at 0.10 → **54.2% against the P208 cap of 50%**. In fraction space the constraint is exact: **0.45 + f ≤ 0.50 → f ≤ 0.05** (1 contract, 49.6% max net, and self-consistent as equity grows since fractions are equity-scaled by P274). **Why 0.10 would be wrong even though the cap BLOCKS the breach rather than allowing it:** XRP would become the asset systematically refused on a one-directional book, so its live behavior would diverge from its forward ledger — silently corrupting the evidence read it exists to earn. Per the P291-D ladder, 0.10/2ct only becomes the right value if the 09-01 tripwire first removes a major (then 39.2% max net).
- **Framing correction:** routing state is NOT the primary lock — the sleeve driver iterates `config.assets` (`['SOL','BTC','ETH']`) and `_pid_to_asset` is built from the assets the sleeve is constructed with, so `config.assets` is the gate a breadth asset hits first. All three locks (config.assets, sizing entries, routing state) are now pinned, each sufficient alone.
- **Honest test-suite accounting:** two inertness assertions were REMOVED (they pinned SYMBOL_MAP absence, which this change deliberately ends) and replaced with stronger ones — the risk shifts from "does an entry exist" to "is it the RIGHT pid" (a wrong pid routes one asset's orders onto another's contract; probe: XRP→SOL's pid → 5 red). Net safety assertions rose, not fell: 2 removed, 2 + 14 added. The config-lock probe wired a fully-armed XRP into a COPIED profile (never the live one) → 6/6 red.
- XRP remains **INERT**; widening still requires moving `config.assets` AND sizing together plus the operator's routing command — a config flip, no code change, which was the point.

### P291. [BUILT 2026-08-17, operator: "can you do all five"] The profit-lever batch — the hold cost was Kraken's BORROW schedule on a venue that posts collateral (and it masked P277's funding), the maker ladder gets one reprice, breadth routing becomes a config flip, and September's criteria + sizing ladder are pre-committed
Operator asked what would boost profit, then instructed all five ranked levers. Four parallel forks (disjoint file ownership; the parent owned main.py + configs/). Tests: `tests/test_p291_{hold_cost,maker_ladder,breadth_readiness,september_criteria,wiring}.py` (107 total); ~20 falsification probes red-then-restored with anchor-uniqueness. Full suite green, gate clean, zero baseline movement.
- **1. VENUE-TRUE HOLD COST — verified, built, DELIBERATELY NOT ARMED.** The live "margin=7" (BTC) / "14.0bps hold" (ETH/SOL) is entirely **Kraken's spot-margin BORROW schedule** (`MARGIN_FEE_BPS` opening + per-4h rollover × `expected_hold_periods_4h`=6), and it fires on ~93% of ticks because the live profile sets `regime_leverage: 2.0` for the two dominant regimes — the dataclass default (1.0) would have zeroed it, so this is a config-driven charge, not a code default. **CDE posts margin as COLLATERAL** (P275 probe: `INTRADAY_MARGIN_SETTING_STANDARD`), not a borrow — the venue never levies it. **The sharper finding: `update_funding_rate` takes `max(rollover, funding)`, and the Kraken rollover (1.0-2.0) beats live funding (0.04-1.9bps/4h) EVERY time — so P277's venue-aware funding has been STRUCTURALLY INERT on the hold path since it shipped** (pinned: adding the funding call changes the hold by exactly 0). We charged a fee the venue doesn't levy, which masked the one it does. Venue-true thresholds: **BTC 26.4→19.1, ETH 44.0→26.7, SOL 40.3→29.0.** **WHY IT IS OFF (`coinbase_venue_true_hold: false`, pinned):** unlike P289's spread fix (which explicitly did NOT re-open ETH/SOL), this crosses BOTH below the 30bps asserted-alpha ceiling — it OPENS two assets at full conviction, on a signal whose measured alpha slope is ~0, 15 days before the 09-01 tripwire that may remove the seat entirely. That is a P141 activation, not a cost correction's side effect. **The fork's own load-bearing design call: an INDEPENDENT gate rather than riding P289's venue memory** — which is already live, so riding it would have armed the asset-opening silently on the next deploy (pinned that the hold gate never reads the spread flag). SOL clears by only ~1bps and re-closes at funding 0.000400 — pinned, so "SOL opens" is never read as unconditional. Fail directions all pinned: unknown venue / never-read funding / **untagged funding** → Kraken schedule (untagged = inert by design, never wrong); cross-asset funding leak refused; negative funding clamped (a subsidy would loosen the gate).
- **2. MAKER REPRICE LADDER — built and LIVE** (`coinbase_maker_reprice: true`). Both live maker attempts posted at the touch, sat the full 45s, and crossed taker. Now: at the half-way mark, if the touch has MOVED PAST our resting order (directional queue-position test — a higher ask on a SELL correctly reads as still-at-front), cancel and re-post ONCE at the new touch. **Inside the same wait budget, never extending it** (a longer wait = a staler signal, P265 class). Enabled rather than default-off because it cannot change WHAT trades and its worst case is byte-identical to today. Invariants pinned: never >1 live post-only; a FAILED cancel refuses both re-post and cross; new `REPRICE_UNKNOWN` → `maker_reprice_unknown_no_cross` inherits the P287 refusal semantics; **never re-post beside an unknown or partial fill** (the fork's addition — sizing a re-post off `filled_size` would need arithmetic on a unit P290 deliberately records verbatim, P219; the venue reconcile sizes the remainder, anti-P139); urgent bypasses entirely. **The fork's regression run caught a P85 in its own work:** `self._maker_reprice` was an undefended read on the live order path whose `AttributeError` the maker handler swallowed into `"FALLBACK"` — every `object.__new__` sleeve (fixtures AND operator scripts like `coinbase_flatten.py`) would have silently crossed taker while logging "maker attempt error". Fixed at the read with `getattr(..., False)`, not by patching fixtures.
- **3. BREADTH ROUTING EXPRESSIBLE, INERT.** Read-only venue probe verified ALL FIVE P262 assets' CDE specs (XRP `XPP-20DEC30-CDE` size 500, ADA `ADP` 1000, LTC `LCP` 5, DOGE `DOP` 5000, BNB `BNB` 1), with BTC/ETH/SOL re-read as a CONTROL reproducing the shipped tables exactly — the new rows were read from the same venue fields, not inferred. The 16 other listed 20DEC30 products are deliberately absent (venue listing is not evidence). **Correction to the P270 note: XRP is NOT thin — 9,361 ct/day ≈ $4.7M, comparable to SOL's 12,658**; BNB (322) and LTC (843) genuinely are. Three independent inertness locks, each pinned (no `SYMBOL_MAP` perp entry → `to_venue_symbol` raises; no fractions/caps in the live profile; routing state unchanged). Fallback tables added FIRST so a later SYMBOL_MAP edit pre-satisfies the one-directional P265h guard (the reverse order breaks it). **`coinbase_set_assets.sh` previously accepted ANY string** — `... BTS` wrote `coinbase_assets:["BTS"]`, routing nothing while looking successful; now unknown/duplicate assets refuse (exit 2, nothing written), breadth assets require typed confirmation after a banner carrying measured volumes + the ~09-15 date + the P197 one-asset-first rule, and a new `HMATS_SET_ASSETS_DRY_RUN=1` guard exists. **Incident, recorded because "the state happened to match" is luck not safety:** a test in that fork invoked the real script and force-recreated the live engine (05:27 UTC); routing content was identical so nothing traded differently, but the warmup clock reset — the P186 class (a test reaching production), root-caused with the dry-run guard rather than papered over (suite 51s → 4.4s is the proof the network half no longer runs). Engine verified after: 11 PASS / 0 WARN / 0 CRITICAL, restarts 0, book flat.
- **4/5. SEPTEMBER CRITERIA + SIZING LADDER, pre-committed before any evidence exists.** `scripts/challenger_seat_check.py` (the P285 instrument pattern, inheriting P287's `*_pulled` dirs and `ic_per_horizon` key fixes) judges donchian/emaens **per CELL**, five conditions: date ≥ 09-16 · lab precondition · ≥30d span with ≥20 directional records · the scorer's own PROMOTE verdict (consumed, never re-derived looser) · **beats the INCUMBENT's forward IC** (the fork's addition: P166 certifies against costs, but a seat swap is comparative). **Scope correction: the P288 dethroning is per-CELL, not per-asset — `emaens` did NOT dethrone ETH (design −0.231 vs +0.088), so `LAB_DETHRONED = {donchian/ETH, donchian/SOL, emaens/SOL}` and emaens/ETH is pinned ineligible.** Exit 3 = **"ELIGIBLE — operator risk-preference decision required"**, never "fire": the P288 crash-dodge caveat is made mechanical (donchian −0.60 / emaens −0.51 vs incumbent −0.28 at a −0.35 bar). Sizing ladder governing sentence: **"size follows certification, never precedes it"** — and the arithmetic produced a non-obvious pre-commitment: at 0.15×3, stepping ONE asset to 0.20 lands net at exactly **0.50 = the P208 cap with zero headroom**, so a lone certification changes NOTHING; room appears only if the tripwire removes an asset (one removed → survivors 0.20, net 0.40; two → survivor to its per-asset cap). Live fractions pinned at 0.15 so a silent raise fails.
- **[P291b — ARMED 2026-08-17, same day, by EXPLICIT OPERATOR INSTRUCTION]** Shown the arithmetic and the asset-opening consequence verbatim, the operator instructed "push and deploy" quoting the finding. `coinbase_venue_true_hold: true` in the live profile; the P291 test pin flipped from must-be-OFF to the DECIDED value (P237 pattern — it now fails on a silent revert too, since either direction is a live-money change). **What this changes live:** the phantom Kraken borrow fee stops being charged on the venue that never levies it, and P277's venue-aware funding becomes the hold cost it was built to be (structurally inert until now). ETH and SOL can pass the alpha gate at **FULL CONVICTION ONLY** — at the common |sig|=0.33 (10bps) both still fail, so this widens the tradeable SET rather than turning the book continuously on; at P274 fractions and ~$10.8k that is ETH ~8ct / SOL ~4ct if both fire, ~0.45 max net vs the 0.50 cap. SOL clears by ~1bps and re-closes at funding 0.000400 — conditional, not structural. **The honest tension, recorded rather than smoothed over:** the fee correction is unambiguously right, but its effect is to trade a signal whose measured alpha slope is ~0, two weeks before the 09-01 tripwire that may retire the trend seat entirely — so the September reads now judge a WIDER book than the one they were designed around. If the tripwire fires, removing the trend injection matters more, not less. REVERT is one config line.
- **Mitigation patterns:** (a) when a cost constant is wrong, check whether it is MASKING a correct one — `max(a, b)` between a wrong-venue fee and the right-venue fee silently retires the right one, and no test of either alone catches it; (b) a cost correction that changes WHICH ASSETS CAN TRADE is not a cost correction — gate it independently of the honest fix it rides beside, or the next deploy arms it by accident; (c) a script that accepts any string as an asset name is a config-corruption surface wearing a validation's name, and its tests must never be able to reach the production half (dry-run guards, not discipline).

### P290. [BUILT 2026-08-17, operator: "fix it"] Sleeve fill-quality logging — the realized-cost instrument the CDE order path never had, so the P289 spread table can eventually be re-derived from fills instead of book probes
P289 recorded that NO realized-fill cost measurement exists for CDE (`fill_quality.jsonl` empty; P169's `[FILL_VS_MID]` sits on the dormant Kraken path). Built observation-only on the single live order path; tests `tests/test_p290_fill_quality.py` (12; sign-convention and Iron-Law-7 probes red-then-restored with anchor uniqueness).
- **Adapter**: read-only `get_order(order_id)` (`_plain`-converted; ANY failure → None + warning — deliberately the OPPOSITE fail semantics of `fetch_open_orders`' post-P287 raise, documented at the site: that raise protects order-path guards; this feeds only a ledger and must never ripple into the path).
- **Sleeve**: `_record_fill_quality` appends `data/fill_quality.jsonl` + one `[FILL-QUALITY]` INFO line per record: decision mid + one decision-time `get_product_book` read (bid/ask/spread context, fail-soft → None — never a fabricated book), fill_avg_price, **signed `realized_slippage_bps` (positive always = paid worse than mid, both sides — a maker BUY at the bid reads NEGATIVE = earned)**, `liquidity` ∈ {maker, taker_cross, direct}, urgent, fees when the payload carries them, `filled_size` verbatim (P219 no-unit-conversion). Hooks: maker fill-truth via one get_order at the left-the-book branch; cancelled-post-only PARTIAL maker fills recorded only when the payload shows filled_size>0; the cross/direct record after placement with ONE immediate get_order — not-filled/unreadable → `status: unresolved` with the order_id and NO fill/slippage fields (absence never reads as a measurement, P2). No polling loops added; every hook try-wrapped and behaviorally pinned (a raising recorder cannot change `execute_target`'s return).
- **Reader** `scripts/fill_quality_review.py` (stdlib-only; added to the image allowlist BOTH halves — Dockerfile COPY + .dockerignore negation, P192): refuses (exit 2) on missing ledger or <20 filled records (P199: no data ≠ verdict); per-asset n / maker share / median+mean slippage by liquidity class / median spread, compared against the CDE table — restated locally with a drift-guard test pinning it equal to `FrictionComponents().CDE_SPREAD_BPS`. **Pre-committed re-derivation rule printed in its own output: the spread constants may only be LOWERED on ≥20 filled legs per asset AND a new P-entry — never automatically.**
- Zero scanner-baseline movement (measured, not estimated — the fork ran the gate). The ledger accrues from the next deploy; fills are rare (BTC-only at full conviction, P289), so the ≥20-leg bar is weeks-to-months out by design — the instrument exists so the evidence accumulates without anyone re-grepping by hand (P230).
- **Mitigation pattern:** when a constant is certified by a PROBE (order-book snapshots), build the realized-measurement instrument in the same breath — a probe is a hypothesis about what fills will cost; only fills settle it, and the fail-semantics of the new read must be chosen per CONSUMER (guard-feeding reads raise; ledger-feeding reads return None), not copied from the nearest sibling.

### P289. [MEASURED + BUILT 2026-08-16, operator-instructed] The few-trades mechanism traced to one comparison, the spread constants re-priced from a live CDE probe (honest in both directions — and it does NOT re-open ETH/SOL), and the P288 challengers wired as forward ledgers
Operator asked why there are so few trades, then instructed the measurement lever + the challenger ledgers. Tests `tests/test_p289_venue_spreads.py` (22) + `tests/test_p289_trend_rule_shadow.py` (14); all falsification probes red-then-restored with anchor-uniqueness (P238).
- **1. The few-trades mechanism, traced with live numbers:** trade scarcity is produced at ONE comparison — asserted alpha (`40 × |sig| × 0.75`, ceiling **30bps** at full conviction) vs per-asset friction thresholds (BTC ~29 / ETH ~43 / SOL ~55-60bps). **ETH and SOL are arithmetically locked out at ANY signal strength** (live log: SOL at 0.83 conviction = 25bps still rejected vs 60); BTC is the single live cell and passes only at |sig|=1.0 by 1-2bps (ALLOW_EPSILON observed). The downstream churn controls are not the binding constraint; the gate is. Both sides of the comparison are asserted constants (P231) — the honest calibration (measured slope ≈ 0) would close the gate entirely, so today's trading level sits between two honest extremes, and the **09-01 tripwire remains the decision date**.
- **2. The measurement lever, exercised: live read-only CDE order-book probe** (2026-08-16, WEEKEND book = worst case, 6 samples/contract): median FULL spreads **BTC 1.58 / ETH 5.26 / SOL 2.65bps** vs the Kraken-era per-leg constants {3/5/10} — SOL's constant was ~4× the measured full spread; the true taker cost is the HALF-spread (0.79/2.63/1.32). New `FrictionComponents.CDE_SPREAD_BPS = {BTC:2.0, ETH:5.5, SOL:4.0}` (full spread rounded conservatively = ~2× buffer, P167) + per-asset `set_spread_venue` memory fed from the existing [VENUE-FEE] resolution (same resolver as fees — no P172 twin), flag `coinbase_venue_aware_spreads` (P201 trio, default false, **true in the live profile by the operator instruction**, annotated with provenance + revert). Failure/unknown venue → Kraken table (overcharge, safe). **Honest in both directions: ETH ROSE 5.0→5.5.** Consequences, test-pinned: BTC threshold ~29→~26bps (the epsilon-marginal pass becomes robust); **SOL ~55→~40 and ETH ~43 — both STILL above the 30bps ceiling, so the honest re-pricing does NOT re-open them**; a future constant edit that would flip that arithmetic fails a test naming it a P141 reopening decision. Also recorded: NO realized-fill cost data exists for CDE (`fill_quality.jsonl` empty; P169's [FILL_VS_MID] lives on the dormant Kraken path) — the probe is the only instrument until sleeve fill-quality logging is built.
- **3. The P288 challengers wired as forward ledgers** (`defense/trend_rule_shadow.py`, P248 self-contained pattern; main.py loop-level fail-soft; prefixes `donchian`/`emaens` at BOTH scorer sites, P192; september_check countdown rows): DONCHIAN-100 and EMA-ENSEMBLE record trend-only claims (bull→+1 else flat, conf=|dir| per P236) per asset to `data/strategy_shadow/{donchian,emaens}_{ASSET}.jsonl`. Canonical labelers live in the defense module with a parity test against `trend_rule_lab`'s (two-file drift guard); Donchian deliberately stays CLOSE-based (the form P288's virgin-era probe actually measured — highs/lows would forward-test a different mechanism, P164/P214 class); EMA warmup <450 bars → flat-with-reason. **30d P166 clocks start at the next deploy (~09-16 read).** Observation-only; a seat/widening decision needs the P288-recorded caveat resolved (both challengers are PARTIAL-certified: transfer-validated with higher out-of-selection totals than SMA200, but ~2× deeper BTC-2018 bear-year drawdown — an operator risk-preference call).
- Baseline: tryexcept 680→682 hand-bumped with attribution (both new guards log). The two new constitution fields use `default_factory`, not the legacy `dict = None` shape (which is a standing mypy finding — no new copies).
- **Mitigation pattern:** "why so few trades" decomposes at the GATE's comparison, and each side must be labeled measurement or assertion — here the only measurement-fixable side (spreads) was measured and fixed, and the honest result is that it does NOT change the answer: the lockout is the asserted-alpha side's, and it already has a decision date. A re-pricing that also TIGHTENS somewhere (ETH) is how you know it was a measurement and not a loosening wearing one's name.

### P288. [RESEARCH + MEASURED 2026-08-16, operator: "is the dynamic-trend failure a design issue? do the sizing-overlay lab"] The design-issue question answered by five measurements — the fitted-forecaster verdict is MARKET PROPERTY (externally replicated), but the audit found two never-tried axes, and one of them produced the campaign's first genuine dethroning candidates
Operator challenged the "every dynamic trend model died" narrative: design issue or market property? Answered by an adversarial three-track research pass (internal tried-vs-untried audit at artifacts; external 2023-26 net-of-cost literature; the authorized sizing lab) plus two gap-closing probes the audit forced. All labs: pre-committed verdicts, design [3000,9100) + pre-design [800,3000) eras only, hard-asserted no validation-era reads, causality construction tests, chassis single-sourced from mechanism_lab (P172), UNIT-control parity refusals. Artifacts: `training/{sizing_overlay_lab,trend_rule_lab}.py`, `training/scripts/{edge_probe_longhorizon,unread_era_probe_rules_p288}.py`, reports in `training/reports/*_p288.json`.
- **1. External literature (13 sources, net-of-cost only): the fitted-forecaster verdict is REPLICATED, not ours alone.** The canonical deep-momentum result (Lim/Zohren/Roberts) beats the simple rule only below 2-3bps costs and reaches at best PARITY with cost-aware training; crypto ML at IC≈0.05 is documented economically dead at ~10bps; MinTRL arithmetic says certifying a ΔSharpe 0.3-0.5 over a rule needs **8-20 YEARS** — our wins-design-eras/dies-unseen-era loop is the predicted signature, and P285b's seed probe is the textbook DSR demonstration. The literature's ONLY plausible beaters are rule-family upgrades: vol-managed sizing, multi-horizon EMA ensembles, properly-tuned jump models (crypto-unproven).
- **2. Internal audit — the narrative was TRUE for fitted forecasters and hid two real gaps:** (a) **no trained model or probe ever had a target beyond 16h** (every campaign: H=4 bars; edge_probe swept 4h/16h only) while the certified books monetize multi-week horizons; (b) **SMA200 never faced its own rule family** (the rules ladder had no EMA-cross, breakout, adaptive-lookback, or vol-managed candidate — despite P270's recorded literature contradiction). Also clarified: P283b's magnitude-discard finding was "quantization is free GIVEN these forecasts", NOT "magnitude carries nothing" in general; and the decisive model kills were re-measurement kills (point estimates), not CI-width statistics — the fold-length power problem (P242) did not cause the verdict.
- **3. Sizing-overlay lab (the operator's ask): BASE STANDS, 0/6 quantized configs earn.** VOLTARGET uniformly destructive (the P263b double-normalization mechanism again); TRENDSTRENGTH wins design eras, surrenders pre-design (P243 class). Granularity is cheap (~3pts on SOL quarters) — the binding constraint is the CAP (BTC's 2ct book has no upsizing headroom). One near-miss recorded not promoted: BTC TRENDSTRENGTH(2.0) CONTINUOUS passes both verdict legs — inexpressible at current equity, 6-config multiplicity attached.
- **4. Long-horizon edge probe: 0/18 cells decidable — the axis is STRUCTURALLY UNCLOSABLE from this data.** At 168h, six years collapse to ~136 effective observations: detection floor IC≈0.17 vs economic bar IC≈0.013-0.023 — every cell lands in the gap BY CONSTRUCTION, and no model capacity changes n_eff. Retroactively explains why the books could only be certified the way they were (virgin era, transfer, forward ledgers). The external group's edge is strictly short-horizon (flips NEGATIVE at 48h+ on BTC/ETH). The 16h anchor cell reproduced the strict-probe record exactly, and the recorded BTC/SOL 16h facts survive the overlap-corrected t.
- **5. Trend-rule family sweep — the FIRST dethroning in the campaign: DONCHIAN-100 and EMA-ENSEMBLE beat SMA200 on ETH and SOL at ~1/4 the turnover** (33-39 flips/era vs 140-143), era-stable on BOTH windows — the property no fitted candidate ever had; their hysteresis removes the SMA200 boundary-flapping that P256's `adjust` was invented to patch. BTC: SMA200 stands. The literature's vol-managed form fails even done the literature's way (rebalance bands + quantization).
- **6. Virgin-era + never-fitted-assets probe of the challengers (P262 machinery, incumbent control reproduced to the 4th decimal): both PARTIAL.** Breadth: 5/5 beats-flat for both, mostly ahead of SMA200 head-to-head. Virgin era: HIGHER totals than the incumbent everywhere (BTC +1.6-1.7 vs +0.9; ETH +2.6-2.7 vs +1.9) — but both FAIL the one cell the certification values most: the **BTC 2018 crash-dodge** (−0.51/−0.60 vs the incumbent's −0.28 and the pre-committed −0.35 bar). The trade-off is characterological: later exits capture more upside and dodge crashes less. **SMA200 keeps its unique certification; the challengers are upgraded to out-of-selection PARTIAL candidates whose path is a forward ledger + an operator risk-preference call** (more upside vs ~2× deeper bear-year drawdown), never a swap on this evidence.
- **VERDICT, in one sentence:** the repeated death of fitted trend forecasters is market property (externally replicated, sample-size-determined, and now shown structurally undecidable at the horizons that matter) — but the audit's challenge was right that the RULE-FAMILY axis was never explored, and its first exploration produced two transfer-validated candidates with a measured risk trade-off, which is more than any trained model ever achieved.
- **Deliberately NOT done:** no runtime change, no seat change, no forward ledgers wired (adding challenger ledgers to the P248 harness is a candidate next step but competes with September's clean accrual — operator call), no validation-era reads anywhere.
- **Mitigation patterns:** (a) "X was tried more than anything else" can be true of a FAMILY and false of the DESIGN SPACE — inventory the axes at the artifacts before asserting exhaustion; (b) when a power analysis shows a cell is undecidable by construction, record THAT as the verdict — it redirects effort better than a FAIL would; (c) an incumbent's certification is a list of PROPERTIES, not a scalar — the challengers beat it on totals and lost on the property the book is deployed FOR (crash-dodging), and only a per-property comparison surfaces that.

Training: the 2× cost defect survived P281 in two importers (see P281 correction); the CoinGlass `_1d` consumption archives were unrefreshable (see P266 correction); `merge_asof` gained a 3-day tolerance (a September rebuild would have stamped weeks of frozen values as `has_external_data`); `--gmm-no-split` can no longer deploy a leaky GMM to models/regime_classifier + `assert_clean_gmm` cross-checks the PROD dir + the legacy shared-GMM fallback refuses (P4); **hetzner_deploy's CI check kept the OLDEST run per workflow — a green-then-red re-run deployed as GREEN** (now newest wins; python3 fallback; missing interpreter refuses); window-ledger counts prefix-matched validation purposes (the recorded P259b spends were invisible to the multiplicity discount); `dsr()` gains the missing skew/kurt inflation term (the recorded P283b/P286 DSRs 0.90/0.66 are pre-fix and mildly optimistic — verdicts unchanged, CI-excludes-zero decides); FLASH_CRASH explicitly disabled (structurally unreachable: 300s window, one sample per 4H tick — real protection is the pipeline bar-over-bar veto, pinned); `[PROOF]` `quant_real`/`dvol_real` now vary (both were constants). One new `noqa: silent-swallow` (binance_flow_feed's `_warn_once` — the P252 method-call-logging blind spot). **[P287g, same day: the first push went CI-red on 8 new mypy findings from the campaign (Optional returns on the new refusal paths, a union-typed carry loop) — fixed at source, not baselined (P284b pattern); local-vs-baseline still shows the P227 environment-fingerprint offset, HEAD-vs-tree diff verified zero new errors.]**
- **Mitigation patterns:** (a) six forks fixing one tree in parallel stays safe under exactly two rules — disjoint FILE ownership and a single committer; every cross-fork dependency was consumed via getattr with a fallback to pre-fix behavior, so batch landing order cannot matter; (b) make the arming decisions BEFORE the fix fan-out, or six sessions answer "is repairing a dead control a repair or an activation" six different ways; (c) an audit-then-fix pipeline earns zero false positives only by verifying each finding at the call site TWICE — once to report it, once to fix it; the campaign's two instruction-level corrections (carry /2 not /6; the ~2× not ~2.4× annualization) both came from the fixer's re-verification.

### P285c. [MEASURED 2026-08-16, same evening] The ensemble certification FAILED — mlp_small is DEAD as a candidate, the early seat is withdrawn, and the trained-model verdict on the current basis is fully restored
`mlp_ensemble_cert.py` (disposition pre-committed before the result): the 10-seed prediction-averaged ensemble through the exact pooled machinery scored **+51.6% net, Sharpe +0.49, CI[−0.72,+1.65] includes zero, DSR 0.29 — FAIL.** The common signal across seeds, once the luck is averaged out, sits far below the certification bar — right where the P285b seed-mean predicted. Executed as pre-committed: **mlp_small is dead as a candidate; the P285 early seat is WITHDRAWN outright** (decision-tree section voided in place; the checker's FRAGILE + ensemble-FAIL state refuses permanently; the seat actuator stays wired but nothing can fire it). The seed-7 shadow ledger keeps accruing as FREE evidence only — its ~09-15 read would now be a surprise, not a confirmation.
- **The evening's arc, stated plainly because it is the campaign in miniature:** morning — first trained model ever certified (P283b); evening — the certification dissolved under its own falsification probes (seed dispersion → FRAGILE → ensemble FAIL), for the cost of a few CPU/GPU hours and zero dollars. This is the P281-recert lesson (ridge_a30's +1.73 evaporating) reproduced on the very next candidate, caught BEFORE money instead of after. **The P281 conclusion is restored without exception: every trained-model class on the current data basis is measured dead under fully honest conditions.** The working models remain the certified rule books (P262 virgin-era + never-fitted-asset evidence); the path to a trained model is NEW INFORMATION (venue-true accrual, maker cost measurement, September reads), not new fits of this data.
- **Mitigation pattern:** when a certification survives every test you ran BEFORE it passed, run the tests you designed AFTER it passed anyway — the seed probe existed only because the pass made its caveat matter, and it killed the pass in an afternoon. A candidate's strongest test is always the one built to break specifically it.

### P285b. [MEASURED 2026-08-16 — the probe paid for itself the day it was built] The 10-seed robustness probe returned FRAGILE: the certified pooled pass is substantially seed luck, and the early-seat criterion is mechanically SUSPENDED
`training/scripts/mlp_seed_probe.py` (pre-committed verdict rules in the docstring BEFORE running): the exact certified config re-fit under seeds 1..10, same windows, same decisive cell, no selection. **Result: seed-7 reproduces the certification to the third decimal (+1.519 vs +1.52 — the machinery is sound), but the median across seeds is −0.088 with 5/10 nonpositive (range −0.92..+1.52); the certified seed is the SECOND-BEST of ten draws → FRAGILE.** The selection-artifact hypothesis gains its mechanism: mlp_small won its p281 fold BECAUSE seed 7 was lucky, and the pooled exam re-read overlapping windows. This is the P281-recert lesson (a celebrated number evaporating under honest re-measurement) caught by our OWN probe BEFORE money — the P283b single-seed caveat, resolved by measurement in hours.
- **Suspension is a MECHANISM, not a memory (P280):** `mlp_seat_check.py` now refuses (exit 2, "SUSPENDED") while the FRAGILE report stands un-answered — pinned by an end-to-end test that reads the REAL repo reports and runs the real checker (falsification-probed red). The decision tree carries the suspension note. The deployed seed-7 shadow keeps running (free, and it is evidence about the deployed instance) but nothing can fire from it.
- **The pre-committed remedy is RUNNING:** `training/scripts/mlp_ensemble_cert.py` — the 10-seed prediction-averaged ensemble (variance reduction with NO selection) through the exact pooled machinery. Disposition written before the result: CI excludes zero → the ensemble becomes the candidate (re-export, ledger discontinuity recorded, clocks restart, criterion re-arms — its own P-entry); CI includes zero → **mlp_small is dead as a candidate and the early seat is withdrawn outright.** Either way the windows are many-times-read and the P260 discount applies — the forward ledger remains the exam. Reports: `training/reports/mlp_seed_probe_p285b.json` (+ `mlp_ensemble_cert_p285c.json` when it lands).
- **Mitigation pattern:** a single-seed certification of a seed-sensitive estimator is a draw from a distribution wearing a measurement's name — probe the dispersion BEFORE the candidate touches a seat, and pre-commit what FRAGILE does to every decision that cited the certification.

### P285. [ADOPTED + WIRED 2026-08-16, operator: "adopt the criterion and wire up"] The mlp early-seat criterion — the certified candidate may take the BTC seat before the full P166 read, because the swap is a direction-source choice on an already-bounded book, not a risk addition

- **Criterion (pre-committed BEFORE any shadow evidence existed, now in `docs/SEPTEMBER_DECISION_TREE.md`):** fire iff ALL of — date ≥ 2026-08-28; mlpshadow_BTC ledger ≥ 14d span with ≥ 40 directional records; **16h shadow IC ≥ 0.0** (a KILL-SCREEN, explicitly weaker than P166 — a negative 2-week trajectory kills the early swap and the model waits for its ~09-15 read); trend still GATE-CLOSED on the latest weekly slope report. Missing data never counts as passing (P199) — `decide()` treats None as failing, pinned.

Exit 3 = FIRE naming the exact config edit; 0 = not yet with per-condition status; 2 = refusal on unreadable inputs. Both fixed 2026-08-16; the suspension gate was never affected.]**

In enforce, the P284 harness's `last_direction()` takes the quant slot by the SAME injection as the trend/regimebook seats (both dicts + dq + P149 sleeve bridge, edge = the same asserted 30bps×|dir| constant — the swap never touches the alpha bar); runs AFTER the regimebook seat so the certified model deterministically wins (pinned).
- **The load-bearing subtlety: coverage gaps must never feed the seat.** `last_direction()` serves only FULL-COVERAGE emits with a 6h staleness bound — a coverage-gap tick records flat in the LEDGER (honest) but does NOT refresh the seat feed, else a feed outage becomes a flatten (P2 absence≠flat, probe-verified); the model's own deadband flat IS a position and seats as 0.0. Missing/stale emit → seat NOT taken, incumbent stands (warn once per asset).
- **What the early seat does NOT change:** the ~09-15 P166 read still governs anything beyond the current book; sizing, caps, stops, the gate, and the promotion standard are untouched. Weekly trajectory reads (september_check) are the kill instrument in the meantime.

### P283b. [R1 EXECUTED 2026-08-16, operator-adopted pooled certification] BTC mlp_small PASSES the pooled exam UNDER THE LIVE DERIVATIVES EXPRESSION — the first trained model ever certified-from-history on fully honest conditions; ETH/SOL fold-winners killed
Operator adopted pooled/lockbox-length certification (the P242 disposition) and asked whether the fold-winners fit the CHANGED (derivatives) system. `training/scripts/pooled_certification.py`: the three FIXED p281 candidates (no new selection), walk-forwarded over all three fold-val windows via the zoo's own machinery, pooled ~5,892 bars, block-bootstrap CI, DSR at the zoo's N=8, reads ledgered — **and the derivatives-fitness question built into the exam**: every candidate scored under 4 expressions {trained-continuous, LIVE sign-quantized (the sleeve's actual ±0.15 bang-bang mapping)} × {±funding carry (P245 convention, Binance-proxy with the P218 caveat)}; the PRE-COMMITTED decisive cell = LIVE expression WITH carry.
