### P283. [PLAN 2026-08-16] The retraining plan — event-driven, four triggers, zero scheduled dates
`docs/RETRAINING_PLAN_2026-08.md` (supersedes TRAINING_PIPELINE_PLAN_V3 as the roadmap). The spine: **retraining is event-driven** — R0 evidence accrual (running: maker fill n≥20, CDE funding, twelve ledgers); **R1 triggered by the operator's P242 certification-criterion decision** (pooled/lockbox-length → re-examine the 3 beat-both families pooled, DSR-adjusted, CPU-minutes; else the zoo is never re-run); R2 triggered by the maker verdict (UNLOCKED → re-price the P166 edge bar to measured cost — explicitly does NOT resurrect the recert incumbent, whose gap is Sharpe-sized not cost-sized); R3 triggered by September passes (signal-basis → lab-laddered tilts; policy-layer question → supervised-first at measured cost; RL last and only after the derivatives-native env + the decision-interval runtime mechanism, both currently hard blocks); R4 calendar (venue-true depth; breadth series buildable early). Hard invariants restated as mechanical. **No scheduled retrain date exists by design** — every information-bearing training event of this round cost minutes once its trigger was real; every scheduled campaign before it burned days re-measuring settled verdicts.

### P282. [REVIEWED + FIXED 2026-08-16] The fresh-mind gate before kickoff — GO-WITH-CHANGES for CPU/forward work, NO-GO for GPU/RL, "enough models" answered by measurement, and the reviewer's four demands executed same-hour
Operator (pre-kickoff): "read what I proposed, start a fresh-mind agent to review whether this round addresses all concerns and produces models that actually generate profit — most importantly, do we try enough models." The P247 discipline ran; the reviewer verified every load-bearing claim at source (four assert_clean_gmm call sites, the three cost-arithmetic fixes agreeing, the futures parquets' 100%-nonzero columns, the recert JSON, decision_interval still zero runtime hits) and rendered:
- **"Do we try enough models?" — YES, and more-models is a CATEGORY ERROR here, quantified:** ~16 families tried with recorded verdicts (books, ridge ×6 expressions, enet, HGB, LGBM, RF, kNN, MLP, GRU, DT, TQC ×3, exit-SAC, composites, meta-labeling, jump, banded, pooled) + 4 excluded with reasons. The untried remainder (TabPFN/FT-Transformer, Kalman/state-space, symbolic/evolutionary, ensembles-of-winners, online learners) each lack a mechanism: the measured nonlinearity residual is **negative** (HGB−ridge −0.012), Kalman is a constrained special case of the already-swept adaptive ridge, search multipliers mechanically worsen DSR (SE(IC)≈0.026 at ~1,500 independent obs — families within ±0.05 IC are indistinguishable; the round's own fresh 196-trial search collapsing CV+1.32→lockbox−0.03 is the live demonstration), and ensembling dead members cannot cross the cost bar. **The binding constraint is information per sample after cost, not hypothesis-class capacity — measured, not asserted.**
- **"Will it produce profit-generating models?" — NO from history, by the round's own decisive read** (the recert collapse); the honest output is the books + twelve forward exams + repaired infrastructure. Overselling flagged and fixed: **the P281 "4/9 beat both baselines" claim was WRONG — the artifact says 3/9** (corrected in place; my count error, caught by the reviewer re-deriving from results.json); the strict edge probe's EDGE_CANDIDATE had NO committed artifact (now `training/reports/edge_probe_strict_p281.txt`, 22 CLEARS lines) — and the reviewer's standing caveat is adopted: **EDGE_CANDIDATE preceded every dead campaign; it is a necessary gate carrying near-zero positive information.**
- **The reviewer's four demands, executed:** (1) **`scripts/maker_fill_review.py`** — the P278 decision bar's standing instrument (the P230 rule: a bar without an instrument becomes "whoever re-greps by hand"); parses `[COINBASE-MAKER]` from the persistent log, refuses a verdict below n=20 legs; **first live reading: 1 maker fill / 3 taker fallbacks (25% at n=4, UNMEASURED — keep accruing)**; taker leg priced at the fee floor so the verdict cannot be flattered toward "unlocked". (2) **CDE-native funding accrual STARTED** — `data/cde_funding_history.jsonl` appends every tick's venue funding from the existing `_coinbase_funding_8h` writer (no archive exists or can be fetched; every unpersisted tick lengthened the P279-D3 depth clock; the deepest derivatives feature is still wrong-venue with a probed sign flip, P218). (3) The 3/9 + artifact fixes above. (4) decision_interval stays a hard Rung-3 block (re-verified zero runtime hits).
- **The one demand that is an OPERATOR decision, presented not taken:** the fold-length CI criterion — P242 proved NO strategy including the baselines certifies at ~1,900 bars, so re-running the zoo under it converts compute to zero bits; the choice (adopt pooled/lockbox-length certification with multiplicity control, or stop re-running the zoo) has been standing since P242 across three campaigns.
- **Mitigation pattern:** the fresh-mind review paid for itself a third time (P247, P260, here) — and its catches were in MY OWN reporting (a miscounted headline, an uncommitted positive artifact), which is exactly why the reviewer must re-derive numbers from artifacts rather than read the ledger's prose.

### P281. [BUILT + MEASURED 2026-08-16] The missing-data layer for the derivatives retrain — the three dead columns filled at FULL depth from an API we already used, the 2× lab-cost defect fixed, and the first honest-economics Rung-0 passed

- **1. `fetch_binance_futures_daily.py`** — futures/um 1d klines → `training_data/futures/{ASSET}_futures_daily.parquet`, the EXACT file `rebuild_pipeline._load_futures_daily` always looked for and never found (the P279 dead-columns defect). Column mapping documented (taker_buy_quote/base → the loader's legacy names; downstream z-score/pct-change are UNIT-FREE so no scale-calibration trap, P219/P224); **causality stamped at BUILD time** (day-D data at the day-CLOSE boundary — the P247 lesson applied at the source, probe-verified); in-progress day dropped (P253c); P266 merge-not-overwrite. **Fetched: all 8 assets, ~2,191 days each (2020-08 → 2026-07), 100% nonzero** — BTC median $6.5B/day taker-buy, 3.3M trades/day.
- **2. Loader REFUSES instead of zeroing** — the silent empty-frame fallback that fed three dead inputs to every model ever trained is now a SystemExit naming the fetcher; the fetch joined `make refresh-data` in dependency order (pinned).
- **3. The 2× lab-cost defect FIXED at source:** `COST_BPS` renamed-in-meaning to ROUND-TRIP (the P166 convention `mechanism_lab` already used); `evaluate_segment` charges half per leg; parity pinned by a test charging one identical trade through BOTH labs. All mechanism-lab-family numbers (P256 adjust, volskip, exit-rule) unchanged — only the supervised zoo's economics corrected. **[P287: "at source" was incomplete — two importers of the renamed constant still charged it FULL per leg (`regime_model_lab`, the P250 roster's lab, and `label_lab`); fixed 2026-08-16 with a three-lab parity test + a repo-wide class sweep.]**

Breadth spot closes backfilled** (fetch_binance_full `--assets`, 6y × XRP/ADA/LTC/DOGE/BNB → raw parquets banked for the xsmom training series); **calbasis `_basis_hist` persisted** (P154 class — the only CDE-native signal's ~3.3-day warmup now survives deploys, round-trip probe-verified; corrupt state degrades to cold start).
- **5. Rebuild + verification:** parquets rebuilt (`--skip-gmm` — the futures columns are not GMM inputs, P215 pairing intact); the three columns now **~99% nonzero with std≈1**; `has_external_data` 8% → ~100%; fv2 100% coverage; parity/causality suites 38/38. **The strict Rung-0 edge probe on the upgraded data: OVERALL EDGE_CANDIDATE — SOL external IC 0.074 t=5.57 +35.7bps after cost, flow_v2 clears** — the doctrine's cheapest gate passed before any training spend.
- **6. The retrain execution LAUNCHED:** `train_supervised_full --assets BTC,ETH,SOL --tag p281_honest_cost` — the supervised zoo re-read at HONEST round-trip cost on parquets with real futures features for the first time (note the deliberate conflation: cost fix + new features land together; the question is "does any cell now clear", not attribution). Results extend this entry when the run lands.
- **Deliberately NOT done:** no manifest change (the new columns were ALREADY manifest members — that is what made them dead, not new; fv2 stays extra); no RL (P280 hierarchy stands); no ETF backfill (lag leak); breadth assets still unfitted beyond trend-only (P262).
- **[RESULTS, same day — the retrain question is now CLOSED at every rung, on the best data the project ever had:]** (a) **supervised zoo re-read (honest cost + real features): 0/9 fold-level promotions but the structure FLIPPED** — models now beat BOTH baselines in **3/9** folds (BTC fold_1 +32.4% vs B&H −48.8%; ETH fold_3 +51.2%; SOL fold_1 +16.7% vs B&H −89.6%) where the old campaigns had 0 [corrected from "4/9" by the P282 fresh-mind review — the artifact says 3; two further folds beat one baseline each]; the universal blocker is now the CI-at-fold-length criterion, the KNOWN statistical-power limit (P242: no strategy incl. the baselines certifies at ~1,900 bars). (b) **fresh 196-trial searches replicated P241's overfit lesson on new data**: BTC's CV+1.32 winner collapsed to lockbox −0.03; ETH's GRU "lockbox +0.00" is VACUOUS (the script's walk-forward skips GRU — a P174 check-that-can't-run, recorded). (c) **THE DECISIVE READ — the P254-authorized targeted re-certification of the ridge_a30 incumbent** (pre-registered config, no search, clean GMM, honest RT cost, single-source machinery, ledgered): **FAIL both — BTC lockbox Sharpe +0.27 CI[−1.15,+1.69] (was +1.28), ETH +0.11 CI[−1.39,+1.47] (was +1.73).** The P254 caveat is resolved by measurement: the incumbent's certification was substantially leaked-era artifact. **The supervised revival path is dead; with it, every trained-model class on the current basis is measured dead under fully honest conditions.** The genuinely positive residue — beats-B&H on both assets, 4/9 fold wins — is real but uncertifiable from history at any window we own; the September forward exams remain the ONLY certification instrument, now proven so at every rung rather than asserted. `training/scripts/ridge_a30_recert.py` + `ds_model_pipeline` fixes (third copy of the 2× cost defect fixed pre-read; clean-GMM gate added; lockbox reads ledgered) committed; reports in `training/reports/` + `models/supervised/*/p281_honest_cost/`.
- **Mitigation pattern:** "missing data" often is not missing — it is an unfetched half of an API already in the tree. Before declaring a data family unavailable, check the OTHER product lines of every archive the project already downloads from. And when an incumbent's certification predates a fixed leak, its re-certification is not optional homework — here the celebrated +1.73 evaporated the day it was re-measured honestly.

### P280. [FIXED 2026-08-16] The clean-GMM prerequisite made MECHANICAL — every trainer and lab now REFUSES leaked or unverifiable regime provenance at startup; and the "is DRL the go-to" question answered on the record
Operator: "make sure, before retraining, we use clean GMM — is DRL the best go-to."
- **The enforcement gap (found by asking "make sure" instead of "did we"):** the clean fits exist on both sides (P221 training, P267 runtime) and the P267 parity guard covers DEPLOY artifacts — but verifying the TRAINING-side `fit_policy` was a HUMAN step (Guide §3 "MUST print: split_aware"; P257 did it by hand). Nothing refused. New `training/splits.py::assert_clean_gmm(asset)`: reads the training-side per-asset gmm_config (the parquet's regime features and that config are ONE artifact set, P215) and **hard-refuses** unless `fit_policy == "split_aware"` — missing config and missing policy also refuse (pre-P200 fits are leaky by construction; P159), and there is deliberately NO override flag (a leaked GMM has no legitimate training use; visualization's `--gmm-no-split` never reaches this gate). Wired at BOTH consumption points: `train_drl_full` main() BEFORE the data load (refuse before any GPU spend) and `train_supervised_full.load_asset` (every lab — regime/mechanism/filter — flows through it). **Falsification run against the REAL artifacts:** BTC passes (split_aware, k=6); flipping fit_policy to full_sample → REFUSED; deleting the key → REFUSED; restored. Tests `tests/test_p280_clean_gmm_gate.py` (7, incl. gate-precedes-data-load ordering).
- **Is DRL the best go-to? NO — the evidence hierarchy is the answer** (P279 §5 stands): (1) the RULE BOOKS are the only certified class (virgin-era + never-fitted-asset transfer, P262); (2) SUPERVISED LINEAR is the only trained class that ever passed a lockbox (ridge_a30, P241) and holds the only live re-entry path (P278's maker-cost condition + the P279-found 2× cost bias in its 0/18 kills — both point toward a cheap re-read, not RL); (3) RL is 0/39 across three clean campaigns with FEE-INDEPENDENT causes (churn behavior, era-fragility, gross losses where IC held — P263c), faces ten env-fidelity gaps (P279) incl. a decision-interval contract no runtime honors, and re-enters ONLY as a policy layer on a September-certified basis with the derivatives-native env built first (P258). The next training dollar goes: breadth series (D0, free) → ridge re-read/revival (D1, CPU) → and RL last, if ever.
- **Mitigation pattern:** "make sure X before Y" is a request for a MECHANISM, not a confirmation — the honest answer to "do we use clean GMM" was "yes, whenever a human remembers §3"; now the trainer itself is the checklist.

### P279. [RESEARCH 2026-08-16] The derivatives-first retraining research — the training system models a world that no longer exists, and the program is a staged rebuild gated on evidence already accruing
Operator ordered comprehensive retraining research given derivatives are the key trade items, with thorough code+doc reading. Three parallel audits (env fidelity, data basis, doctrine), every load-bearing claim re-verified directly (P228). Full report: `docs/research/RETRAINING_DERIVATIVES_RESEARCH_2026-08-16.md`. Headlines:
- **Ten env-vs-venue fidelity gaps**, three transfer-invalidating: funding carry NEVER reached the DRL env (P245 wired it into the LABS only — the policy optimizes a different objective than the book settles in); the alpha-gate/stop/flatten regime makes deployment off-policy by construction (exit events of training-probability zero); and **`decision_interval` has NO runtime counterpart** (grep: zero matches outside the trainer) — recorded as a HARD Rung-3 prerequisite: no di-trained checkpoint may shadow-deploy until a runtime hold mechanism exists, else its shadow IC measures a different policy than was trained (P214 class).
- **The parquets are a spot dataset with a derivatives garnish:** ~118/135 columns are Binance-spot functions; the real derivatives content is one full-depth wrong-venue funding column, two 8%-coverage CoinGlass columns — and **THREE ALL-ZERO CONSTANTS** (`taker_ratio_zscore`/`tradecount_zscore`/`taker_vol_momentum`: `training_data/futures/` never existed and the loader silently backfills 0.0 — every model ever trained consumed three dead inputs). Three price bases coexist (train=Binance spot, live features=Kraken spot, monetized=CDE dated contracts); term-basis drift is unpriced in every backtest ever run.
- **Lab cost-convention split, VERIFIED at both sites:** `train_supervised_full` charges COST_BPS per SIDE (=12bps RT BTC) while `mechanism_lab` charges it as ROUND-TRIP (6bps, matching the P166 gate). Internally consistent each; cross-lab 2× apart — and the 0/18 supervised kills were priced at DOUBLE the gate's cost, biased AGAINST models. Disposition: unify naming + cheap CPU re-read at honest cost, composing with the P278 maker re-read.
- **Depth reality:** of the new families only **breadth/xsmom closes (~6y) are walk-forward-backfillable today — and unbuilt**; liq-4h needs ~5y of P266 accretion; ETF backfill would IMPORT the reporting-lag leak (P164 class — forward ledger only); calbasis is forward-only and its `_basis_hist` is RAM-only (persist, P154 class).
- **The staged program (§5 of the report):** D0 now/free (zero-column fix + loader refusal, cost-convention unify + supervised re-read, calbasis persistence, BUILD the breadth training series, keep accruing maker + P166 evidence); D1 = the P278 supervised revival iff measured RT ≤ ~3bps; D2 = a derivatives-native env (the ten gaps as its requirements spec) ONLY after September certifies a basis (P258); D3 = venue-true families reach depth by forward accretion. NOT in the program: RL retrain on the current basis, fitting breadth assets (burns P262), ETF backfill, gate loosening before the fill rate is measured.
- **Mitigation pattern:** "do we need to retrain" decomposes into whether the MODEL, the ENVIRONMENT, or the DATA is stale — here the model verdicts stand, while the environment and data quietly stopped describing the venue; retraining without fixing those would measure the wrong world more precisely.

### P278. [VALIDATED 2026-08-16] The retrain question re-examined under maker economics — verdict NO retrain now, but the cost-bar arithmetic found a REAL crossing with a dated, free-to-measure re-entry condition
Operator asked to validate whether the derivatives work needs a retrain. Verdict from the recorded evidence + one new measurement:
- **Nothing that killed the model families has changed.** The 0/39-fold verdicts (TQC ×3 campaigns, supervised zoo, meta-labeling, banded, pooled — P200/P241/P249/P250/P258/P259b/P263) died on CHURN BEHAVIOR, ERA-FRAGILITY and DIRECTION QUALITY as much as on cost — none of those is touched by anything shipped since. The P258 re-entry rule stands: RL only with a new formulation premise. The clean GMM deploy (P267) explicitly did not void the retrains; the breadth assets stay DELIBERATELY UNFITTED (fitting them burns the P262 virgin-evidence property that certified the trend mechanism).
- **The one genuinely new fact: maker economics MOVE THE COST BAR ACROSS THE MEASURED SIGNAL.** Every campaign priced 3bps taker (6bps RT). The P166 required-IC at 16h (σ=107bps, 2× margin): RT 6bps → **IC 0.134** (the bar every probe failed); RT 3bps (half-maker mix) → **IC 0.067**; RT ~1bps → 0.022. The strict edge-probe ICs (BTC 0.089 / ETH ~0.08 / SOL ~0.09 at 16h) sit BELOW the taker bar and **ABOVE the half-maker bar** — the settled "signal below the cost of trading it" verdict is CONDITIONAL on a fee regime that P270 just changed.
- **Why this still does not justify a retrain TODAY:** the crossing is conditional on the maker FILL RATE, which is unmeasured (n=1 live attempt, 0 fills, thin weekend book). Retraining before the effective RT is measured would repeat P269's mistake in reverse — GPU spent on assumed economics. The fill-rate evidence is FREE: every sleeve order logs `[COINBASE-MAKER]` filled-vs-fallback.
- **The dated re-entry condition (recorded so it cannot be re-derived or forgotten):** after ~2-4 weeks of maker ledger evidence, compute the effective RT (maker_fills×~0.5bps + taker_falls×6bps weighted). **IF effective RT ≤ ~3bps: the justified move is the SUPERVISED revival, not RL** — re-fit ridge_a30-class on the clean parquets (the exact P254-recorded revival path: "re-fit + re-certification on the clean parquets first"), judged at the MEASURED cost, through Rung-3 shadow. RL stays dead regardless (behavioral/era failures are fee-independent — P263c measured BTC losing GROSS even where its IC held). The P166 gate itself stays at 6bps×2 until the fill rate is measured — overcharging is the safe direction (P167).
- **Mitigation pattern:** a "no edge after costs" verdict is a joint claim about the signal AND the fee regime — when the fee regime changes materially, re-run the bar arithmetic before re-running any training. Here the arithmetic cost nothing and converted "retrain? no, never" into "no — until a specific free measurement lands, then supervised-only, at the measured cost".

### P277. [BUILT 2026-08-16, operator-instructed "full enhancement"] Five new signal-family forward ledgers + the venue-funding hold-cost activation — the coverage audit's every plausible gap, shipped as exams
Operator rejected minimum-sufficient coverage and instructed implementing ALL identified enhancements. The 2026-08-16 taxonomy audit (external, evidence-graded) drove the roster; everything ships OBSERVATION-ONLY through the standard P166 exam (Iron Law 7), probed before design (P218: stablecoin exchange-balance endpoints 500 on our plan → the mcap-delta PROXY is used and LABELED on every row; OI v4 history 400 → fed from the tick's own market_data; CDE dated contracts quoted for all three assets). Module `defense/enhancement_shadows.py`; tests `tests/test_p277_enhancement_shadows.py` (19; four falsification probes red — TWO of which first indicted their own test/probe inputs before the pins could be trusted: the constant-slope fixture never reached the z-branch, and the "contango" fixture was actually BACKWARDATION (near 63360 > far 63045, the real market's shape), so the level-based probe had been testing the branch the fixture never took. The P238/P272 lesson, twice more).
- **The five families:** `stablecoinflow` (BTC/ETH — z of last completed day's aggregate USDT-mcap delta; the paper's variable is exchange inflows, arXiv 2411.06327 — every row carries `basis: mcap_delta_proxy` so the exam judges the proxy honestly); `oidiv_confirm`/`oidiv_fade` twins (OI↑→follow price / OI↓→fade price — disjoint conditions, the P219 twin pattern, fed from the tick's `oi_change_24h_pct` stash); `calbasis` (CDE's OWN dated contracts vs the 20DEC30 perp-style: annualized slope, signal = z of the slope vs its trailing readings — **change-based by pinned design**, because crypto's near-permanent contango makes any level-based form a constant short; interesting live fact: the current term structure is mildly BACKWARDATED); `xsmom` (cross-sectional 30d momentum across the 8 book assets, long-top-2/short-bottom-2, **all-flat below 6 rankable assets** — 3 correlated majors are not a cross-section, pinned); `eventfilter` (FOMC/CPI/Sunday-thin windows — STATIC 2026 calendar carrying an OPERATOR-VERIFY warning; ledger claim = gated direction zeroed in windows; **no enforce flag exists yet by pinned design** — enforcement is its own P-entry).
- **Wiring:** loop-level fail-soft tick fed from what the tick already holds (regimebook's `_last_closes` stash [P277 1-line addition] → xsmom + 24h price change; per-asset `_enh_oi_stash`; the driver's final gated dirs; `_cde_quote_map()` = one get_products call, near-leg ≥10d to expiry, far = >1000d). Prefixes registered at BOTH scorer sites (P192). All 30d clocks start at this deploy (~09-15).
- **The one live change: `coinbase_venue_aware_funding: true`.** P218 built it and proved it inert on the DIRECTION consumers — but the coverage audit surfaced what P218 missed: the alpha gate's hold-cost arithmetic (`FrictionComponents.update_funding_rate` → `_margin_cost_bps`) charges whatever rate `market_data` carries, so with the flag OFF the sleeve's hold cost was priced on KRAKEN's funding. Now routed assets charge the venue that holds the position (~1bp today — small, honest, tightening-direction; wrong-way failure keeps Kraken's rate).
- **Coverage verdict now on record:** every taxonomy family with strong-or-moderate evidence at this scale is live or examined; the rationally-skipped set (options/VRP, delta-neutral carry, sub-minute lead-lag, dated-futures spreads AS TRADES) is skipped with the evidence cited; sentiment remains deliberately over-built and zero-weighted. **Twelve candidates now sit P166 exams** (regimebook raw/adj, derivflow twins, ma_filter, volskip, etfflow, breadth books, + the five P277 families).
- **Mitigation pattern:** "implement everything" is compatible with discipline exactly one way — every addition enters as a ledger with its evidence grade written on it, and the ones the literature couldn't certify (oidiv, calbasis, xsmom) are precisely the ones where the forward exam IS the first real evidence anywhere. And when a falsification probe stays green twice in one batch, the probe is auditing the TEST — check what branch the fixture actually reaches, and what regime (contango vs backwardation) the fixture's numbers actually encode.

### P276. [FIXED + MEASURED 2026-08-16] The P275 gaps closed — the roster guard's FIRST enumeration caught a live liquidation door, and the profit-taking "gap" died honestly in the lab
Operator: "make a plan and fix all gaps." The four P275 findings, each dispositioned by fix or measurement:
- **1. The veto-roster guard — and its first catch.** New `_SLEEVE_FLATTEN_INTENDED_VETOES` roster (main.py, test-enforced, no runtime consumer) + `TestVetoRosterCompleteness`: every `veto_reason` string literal in the write-site corpus (main.py + integration_v36 + trade_gate, comment/docstring-stripped) must match HOLD ∪ VENUE_NA ∪ FLATTEN-INTENDED — **a NEW veto writer goes RED until its author classifies it** (closing P275 #4: unclassified writers silently inherited liquidate-the-sleeve semantics; the P239 guard only caught renames). Anti-vacuity floor (>30 literals extracted, P174) + anti-rot mirror (every roster entry needs a live write site). **The first enumeration immediately found `DISCONNECTED_MID_TICK` (main.py:~14747): the SECOND Kraken-disconnect door — P253 #1 fixed the early-return door, this in-decide writer was missed, so a KRAKEN API disconnect still liquidated the COINBASE book. Now in `_SLEEVE_HOLD_VETOES`.** Borderline writers ([PATCH-1] Schema fail, [BUGFIX M6]) classified AS-CURRENT with review notes — flipping semantics needs its own P-entry, not a side effect of building a guard. Known limitation inherited from P265b: fully-runtime-composed reasons are invisible to the scan (the NO_TRADE subtype family, enum-pinned separately).
- **2. SHORT_CONTROL hardened:** `"Alpha gate"` added to `_SC_PROTECTED_VETOES` — the override (live on the sleeve per P275 #3) can now never clear the ECONOMICS veto and admit a short whose edge failed the gate. Pure tightening; pinned.
- **3. FORCE_AGGRESSIVE annotated at the write site** (integration_v36): both fields it writes are consumed only by the Kraken body — no-op live, kept for a revival, ABORT+CLOSE half remains live.
- **4. The profit-taking gap CLOSED BY MEASUREMENT, not by building.** `training/exit_rule_lab.py` (chassis imported, design+pre-design only): **trailing exits GUT the trend books** (BTC +1.07→+0.14 design at the best k; SOL base stands; ETH era-fragile) and **ETH scale-out subtracts** (+0.088→+0.025). The verdict matches first principles: profit-taking truncates the right tail trend books live on — their hold-until-regime-flip exit IS the edge. The sleeve deliberately keeps NO profit-taking logic; the P275 "gap" is recorded as a measured NON-gap (the P249 meta-labeling shape: a textbook-correct technique failing because its premise is absent). No validation read spent — nothing passed the two-era bar.
- Tests: +5 in `tests/test_sleeve_gated_intent_p206.py` (42 in file; three falsification probes incl. injecting a brand-new unclassified writer → red). 
- **Mitigation pattern:** a guard's first enumeration over a previously-unenumerated surface is itself an audit — build the roster BEFORE trusting any "we covered that class" claim (P253/P265b covered the disconnect class twice and this door survived both). And when an audit names a "missing capability", the fix ladder starts with MEASURING whether the capability would earn — here the lab killed it for minutes of compute, where porting the Kraken exit stack would have deployed a measured-negative strategy.

### P275. [AUDITED 2026-08-16] The operator's venue-binding validation — every strategy/risk/exit component classified BINDS-SLEEVE vs KRAKEN-ONLY at the call site, Coinbase account settings probed live, four findings dispositioned
Operator asked (in Chinese) to validate the Coinbase contract/margin/perp-portfolio settings, whether the code exists, and whether any strategy/agent/risk serves ONLY Kraken. Account probe + a full call-site audit agent (re-verified before recording, P228):
- **Account settings (live probe):** contracts BIP/ETP/SLP-20DEC30-CDE (0.01/0.1/5, venue=cde) are EXPIRING **2030-12-20** — no roll until then (P265h's drift guard covers the eventual roll); margin `INTRADAY_MARGIN_SETTING_STANDARD`; balance summary confirms the P153/P265a denomination split live: CBI spot $457 / CFM cash $0 / **futures_buying_power $3,772.87 ≈ sleeve equity** — deposits must reach the PERP PORTFOLIO to count (spot→perp internal transfer is a required step of the P274 wire).
- **The verified classification (agent, all call-sites read):** BINDS-SLEEVE — the entire risk core (alpha gate + hold band, P0/trade_gate via intent classification, fuse both halves, SOTA/35% kill, LIVE 25% halt, sleeve 15% halt, P208 net cap, P210 caps, FastRisk sleeve branch, P197/P205 stops, daily-loss kill, FORCE_FLAT sleeve section) + signal layer (all 26 agents reach the sleeve ONLY through `decide()`→gated intent). KRAKEN-ONLY (dormant, most with sleeve-native replacements): the EXIT STACK (soft_stop/gambler_exit/exit_alpha partials/adaptive_stop — all gate on `_get_effective_position_state`, which in LIVE reads the Kraken book), Exit-DRL (doubly dead), MAX_HOLD, CORR-0, tranche T1-T4, AC-0/1/2/5, thesis budget, FLIP_GATE (→flip_persist), P168 cooldown (→P232), `place_stop_loss` (→ensure_protective_stop), PA executor (→P270 maker ladder), the unit_system sizing stack (→P273/P274), P144 GlobalExposureCap (→P208), AuthorityChain/LeverageGuard. PARTIAL: deadlock (ABORT+CLOSE flattens the sleeve; FORCE_AGGRESSIVE writes fields the sleeve never reads — the P170 hazard is MOOT post-cutover, recorded), smart beta (gate-mult binds, size-mult discarded).
- **Findings dispositioned:** (1) execution_service's "TWO-SLEEVE / NO venue fork / Kraken trades all assets normally" comment — falsified by P152 twenty lines below it, stood two months — CORRECTED in place; (2) FORCE_AGGRESSIVE-is-a-no-op recorded above; (3) **SHORT_CONTROL's veto-override IS live on the sleeve** (clears non-protected vetoes on short intents → a would-be flatten becomes an executed perp short) — verified BOUNDED: `_SC_PROTECTED_VETOES` covers P0_SAFETY/fuse/drawdown/correlation/TRADE_GATE/NO_TRADE, and override_veto only arms for short-block-class vetoes; **hardening candidate recorded: the alpha-gate veto string ("Alpha gate") is NOT in the protected set** — safe only while override_veto's arming stays narrow; (4) the standing hazard that every NEW veto writer anywhere inherits flatten-semantics on the sleeve by default (the P239 drift guard covers renames, not new writers) — re-recorded; mitigation remains P265b's rule: every new veto writer must be checked against `_SLEEVE_HOLD_VETOES`.

The old "±1 contract can't do partials" excuse WEAKENS under P274 — at ETH 3-8ct partials are expressible. A sleeve partial-exit design is now a legitimate candidate, through the lab ladder first, not a port of the Kraken exit stack (whose exit_alpha config was never validated on this venue).
- **Mitigation pattern:** "does X serve the venue that trades" has three different answers per component — the input it reads, the field it writes, and whether anything on the live path consumes that field — and a control can pass two of three and still be decorative (FORCE_AGGRESSIVE passes input+write, fails consumption). Classify all three, at the call site, per component.

### P274. [BUILT 2026-08-16, operator-instructed] Equity-scaled sizing — the idle-$7k decision made executable: a deposit now deploys itself under the standing caps, with zero behavior change until the money actually moves
Operator: "we should use the idle $7k kraken usd." The wire itself is physically the operator's (Kraken → Coinbase → perp portfolio; steps below); what the SYSTEM owed was that arriving capital gets USED — the P273 fixed {1,3,1} contract book would have left a doubled sleeve at ~0.15× net, recreating at the new equity the exact under-utilization P273 fixed at the old one. Tests +5 in `tests/test_p272_advisory_and_caps.py` (24; two load-bearing pins falsification-probed red).
- **Mechanism:** `coinbase_target_fraction_by_asset` (P201 trio + ctor; live `{BTC:.15, ETH:.15, SOL:.15}`) → `_sized_contracts(asset)` = floor(fraction × `sleeve_equity_usd()` / 1-contract notional). **ONE function is both the target and the per-asset contract cap** (cap == target at any equity, P172 single-source) — without the cap following the size, can_trade's fixed {1,3,1} caps would block the scaled targets the moment capital arrived (caught at design time; pinned). Ctor clamps fractions to ≤0.25 (the largest standing P210 per-asset cap — a fat-fingered 1.5 must not size a 10× book).
- **The activation-safety identity, pinned by test:** at the activation equity ($3,775), 0.15 fractions reproduce the P273 book **exactly** ({BTC:1, ETH:3, SOL:1}) — behavior is byte-identical until capital moves; at ~$10.9k the same config yields {BTC:2, ETH:8, SOL:4} ≈ 0.45× max net, still under the 0.50 net cap and every P210 fraction cap, all of which are equity-denominated and scale automatically.
- **Fail-safe direction:** unreadable equity or an unpriceable contract → `None` → the FIXED contract book (sizing is never fabricated from missing data, P2/P208; staleness resolves toward the smaller known-safe book — including in can_trade, where an unpriceable tick binds the FIXED cap).
- **The operator transfer (recorded; only they can execute):** Kraken: buy USDC with the USD → withdraw USDC to the Coinbase USDC deposit address (minutes, ~$1 network fee; ACH via bank is the slow alternative) → in Coinbase, move the funds from spot into the **perpetuals/derivatives portfolio** (the sleeve reads CFM portfolio equity). The system needs NO config change on arrival — the next `snapshot()` reads the new equity and the next managed tick sizes to it. Equity-feed effects are benign by construction: P0 combined equity RISES (a deposit reads as a gain against the anchors — conservative direction; the fuse window sees a positive delta once).
- **Mitigation pattern:** a "use the capital" instruction has two halves — the wire (theirs) and the elasticity (ours). Fixed-size configs are correct exactly once; any sizing decision expressed in absolute units silently expires the day the denominator changes. Express policy in fractions, derive units at the edge, and pin the identity that makes the switch a no-op at the current state.

### P273. [ACTIVATED 2026-08-16, operator-instructed] Capital utilization made real — sized per-asset targets fill the STANDING policy caps (vol-parity {BTC:1, ETH:3, SOL:1}), and the heartbeat now tells the whole capital truth
Operator: "make sure the system actually uses as much money as possible, the sharpe is as big as possible, and the alpha/beta are accurate as much as possible." The three answers, each honest about what it is:
- **1. Sized targets (the money question).** The driver's `target_for_signal` emitted flat ±1 forever — at ~$3.8k that deployed a 0.32× gross max with ETH at 5% of equity per contract. New `CoinbaseSleeve.target_for(asset, dir)`: sign × the configured per-asset size; `manage_to_signal` and ALL THREE main.py driver-adjacent reads (ma_filter raw target, cooldown sent-flat, stop-reconcile intent) use it — a reader on the unsized function would reason about a different book than the driver trades (P2 class; pinned `target_for_signal(` absent from main.py). **Live values {BTC:1, ETH:3, SOL:1} = vol-parity ($5.0/$6.3/$6.0 per-4H-bar risk) chosen to FILL the standing caps, not widen them**: max all-same-direction book ~0.42× net vs the recorded 0.50 net cap; P208/P210/can_trade all still gate independently; the deadband flatten and P85 fallback survive sizing (truth-table pinned). **This is the P272-reserved operator flip, given explicitly** — the alpha gate still decides WHETHER (it currently vetoes ETH/SOL most ticks), this changes HOW MUCH. FastRisk REDUCE_50 becomes expressible on ETH (3→1). Revert: remove the config key (falls back to flat ±1).
- **2. Sharpe (answered, not re-implemented):** leverage is Sharpe-invariant; the levers are already running — certified-signal advisory over the passive 85% (P272), Sep 1 negative-component removal, Sep 7-15 certified additions, then denominator repair. Utilization now shows in the heartbeat (`gross/net % of sleeve equity`) so "is the money used" is a read, not a guess.
- **3. Alpha/beta accuracy:** beta + per-agent IC instruments already cron weekly (P230/P235). The heartbeat gains `Unmanaged (NOT in equity/risk)` — Kraken non-USD holdings valued at market (~12.3k XRP) + idle USD — **DISPLAY-ONLY by hard design: folding $12k into counted equity would corrupt every persisted drawdown anchor (P261 class)**; `_unmanaged_holdings_text` is 1h-cached, fail-soft, and renders valuation failure as failure, never as $0 (P2). **The gate-alpha fork stated for the record: the gate's alpha number becomes fully honest only when the calibrated slope (≈0) replaces the asserted 40bps constant — which closes the gate. That is exactly the Sep 1 tripwire; making it "accurate" early = going flat two weeks early, offered and not taken.**
- Tests: +4 in `tests/test_p272_advisory_and_caps.py` (19 total; sized-target truth table + driver-uses-sized-targets both falsification-probed red); the P251 cooldown pin updated to the sized call; the P272 not-in-live pin became the decided-values pin (P237 pattern, operator flip given). Baseline 670→671 hand-bumped (the per-symbol ticker guard).
- **Mitigation pattern:** "use more money" decomposes into WHETHER (the gate, untouched), HOW MUCH (sizing, this change), and WHAT IT'S MEASURED AGAINST (display truth, this change) — conflating them is how sizing instructions turn into gate loosenings. And when a sizing function grows an asset dimension, grep every caller of the unsized form: three readers in main.py would have silently reasoned about a ±1 book while the driver traded ±3.

### P270. [BUILT 2026-08-15] The profitability batch — maker-first execution (default OFF), the ETF-flow forward ledger, and the volskip entry filter that survived all three eras

Timeout+failed-cancel REFUSES the cross (`maker_cancel_failed_no_cross` — two live orders for one delta is the P265 double-order class). `urgent=True` skips the ladder entirely and is passed at BOTH emergency call sites (fast-risk watchdog, FORCE_FLAT) — an emergency exit must never wait to save 3bps. Absent from the live profile (pinned); enabling is an operator decision with its own P-entry. At 0bps maker vs 3bps taker this is the largest CERTAIN improvement available — pure cost, no era-risk.
- **2. ETF-flow forward ledger (`defense/etf_flow_shadow.py`, P248 self-contained pattern).** CoinGlass v4 (the plan already paid for) serves `etf/{bitcoin,ethereum}/flow-history` — probed: 667/529 daily rows with per-ETF breakdown. Design-era sanity check (last 60d untouched): sign(flow_t)→ret_{t+1} monotone, +91bps after inflow days / −111 after outflow — **recorded as HYPOTHESIS ONLY: ETF flows finalize with a T+1 reporting lag, so the backtest plausibly contains a reporting-lag leak.** The harness is the honest instrument: per 4H tick it records the direction implied by what the API ACTUALLY serves (newest COMPLETED UTC day only — the API's last row is today and updates intraday, the P253c class; >3d stale → flat-with-reason, weekend-aware). ETH-only overlay ledger `regimebook_volskip_ETH.jsonl` wired into the P248 harness (constant exported threshold 0.018769 = the read-time expanding q0.80, the P259 constant-σ divergence pattern; exits NEVER gated, blocked flips degrade to flatten per P195/P236; **live single-step machine pinned bar-for-bar against the lab's batch filter** — the P164/P214 parity class).
- **Also from the probes, recorded:** CDE lists ~18 more 20DEC30 nano perps incl. ALL FIVE P262-certified never-fitted assets (XRP/ADA/DOGE/LTC/BNB) plus gold/palladium futures — breadth is the evidence-backed expansion (P262: trend beats flat 5/5 on unread assets) but volumes are thin and widening is an operator decision needing per-asset books; the early trajectory read of the existing ledgers (informational, scorer-blocked from promoting at 10d) shows BTC regimebook accruing positive mid-horizon IC at noise-level n, derivflow still incoherent, ETH/SOL books legitimately flat.

Worst case if wrong: one order's fill delayed by ≤45s before crossing at the old taker price. REVERT: set false + redeploy.
- **Mitigation pattern:** the probe-first discipline paid three times in one batch — post_only enforcement (which made the maker ladder safe to build), the ETF endpoint's real path (v3 404s, v4 serves), and the reporting-lag caveat (which demoted a +6.5-Sharpe backtest to a hypothesis and let the forward ledger be the judge). And when a mechanism FAILS a lab (P256's volfilter), record which FORM failed — the winning form here was the same idea on the opposite tail, entry-gated instead of position-gated.

### P269. [FIXED 2026-08-15] The docs-vs-implementation cross-check ("fix all") — the canonical training commands trained the WRONG formulation, a second undocumented live GMM was overwriting the calibrated posterior, and a latent fallback classified assets with another asset's model
Operator ordered a cross-check of the GMM + DRL-retraining docs against code, then "fix all". Two audit passes found drift in BOTH directions — docs prescribing retired procedure, and code silently diverging from documented design. All fixed, pinned by `tests/test_p269_crosscheck_fixes.py` (19; six load-bearing pins falsification-probed red — after first catching my probe harness itself running under an interpreter with no pytest, rc=1 reading as "red": the P159 shape INSIDE the falsification tooling; probes re-run under `sys.executable` with green-before-probe asserted):
- **1. Every scripted DRL entry point trained the retired formulation.** `make drl` and `run_training.run_drl` both lacked `--decision-interval 4` (training the every-bar churn design three campaigns measured dead, P242/P258), and the Makefile additionally forced `--lr 3e-5` + `--buffer-size 2000000` (pre-Optuna lr; the buffer the Stage-9.7 fix capped) with a FIXED tag `makefile_default` — whose second run would restore cached folds and report stale numbers as if it trained (P200 gotcha). CLAUDE.md's own Training Commands section carried the bare no-flag command. All now carry the full honest-flag set with per-run fresh tags.
- **2. The 500K buffer cap only existed under `--config`.** The cap sat inside `if args.config:`, so any plain CLI `--buffer-size` (exactly what the Makefile passed) flowed uncapped into the model. Moved out; unconditional.
- **3. A SECOND live GMM nobody documented was overwriting `regime_confidence`.** `market/regime_navigator.py` carries an ONLINE-FIT 6-component GMM (legacy STRONG_* vocabulary), and main.py:~7750 let its confidence max-overwrite the per-asset GMM's calibrated posterior in both signal dicts — two classifiers' confidences silently fused under one key, partially defeating the P267 deploy's calibrated ~0.90-0.92 posteriors (any tick the online GMM was more confident, a saturated old-style number won). The overwrite's own comment claimed it "wires into the alpha gate" — regime confidence is not on the gate path at all. RETIRED; the navigator keeps its namespaced `p0_regime`/`p0_regime_confidence` keys.
- **4. Latent cross-asset model application.** main.py's loader sets the "legacy fallback" GMM fields from the FIRST loaded per-asset model (BTC), and `_predict_gmm_regime`'s else-branch reads them — so an asset whose artifact pair went missing would be silently classified with BTC's GMM and BTC's regime names. Now: per-asset era + missing artifact → loud warn-once + `return None` (the documented ADX-proxy fallback), never another asset's model. The P4 mixed-artifact shape one level up.
- **5. Reward tables were blind to the clean vocabulary.** `STEADY_UPTREND` (in ETH/SOL's k=7 P221 vocabulary) was in neither `BULL_REGIMES` nor `POSITION_BIAS` — the P184 unmapped-regime no-op path, arriving via a vocabulary change instead of a dtype. Added (+0.08 bias, matching its 3.7%-of-ticks mild-bull census); `NEUTRAL_DRIFT` kept at 0.0 for old-parquet runs.
- **6. Docs corrected at the claim level:** TRAINING_GUIDE_V2 §2's phantom "convert 4h → _1d (see P200 session notes)" step replaced with the real fetchers (`fetch_binance_funding.py` + the P266 merge note) — no converter ever existed and the referenced notes are not in the repo; §5 "runtime hardcodes 1008" corrected (accepts 126/139 stacked, P241-P1b); §7 Rung-3 now states the two glossed-over mechanics (BEST_FOLDS hardcoded fold_3 in THREE files with no tag-aware loader, and the P215 atomic GMM+checkpoint deploy); Part4 v10 + training/README.md + README_V4.md got historical banners (Part4's header still claimed "DRL now DECIDE"); UNIFIED's Best-of-N regime-multiplier table corrected (code INVERTED it 2026-04-22: momentum in MOMENTUM_RALLY is ×0.7 not ×1.3) and its k=8 rows annotated to the clean k=6/7/7; METHODOLOGY_BRIEF gained the funding-rule reconciliation note (the regimebook funding legs are the rule's own "until forward data" test, not a violation).
- **Also:** `run_training`'s gmm step repointed train_per_asset_gmm.py → `scripts/rebuild_pipeline.py` (a standalone GMM refit against existing parquets breaks P215's one-versioned-set rule; the rebuild fits GMMs + regenerates parquets in one run) — the P189 orchestrator test updated to pin the new contract.
- **Mitigation pattern:** the canonical command block in the project's own instructions file is a deploy surface — it was three flags behind the guide it sat next to, and anyone (including a future session) following it would have burned a GPU-day re-measuring a settled verdict with the wrong economics. When a doc names a procedure, pin the COMMAND'S load-bearing flags with a test, not just the prose.

### P267. [DEPLOYED 2026-08-15, operator-instructed] The clean split-aware GMMs finally reached the RUNTIME — closing the P215 three-vocabulary divergence — with a vocabulary-parity guard so the next refit cannot drift silently
Operator asked whether the clean GMM was ever deployed (it was not) and whether that voids the retrains (it does not), then instructed the deploy. The artifact trail, verified at all three locations: training-side fits clean (P221, k=6/7/7 split-aware); **operator-local `models/regime_classifier/` clean since 2026-08-08 00:20** (P221's deploy-copy step ran LOCALLY and its "GMM deployed" log read like a live deploy — it never was); **server host + container still served the pre-P200 full-sample k=8/7/7 fits** (Apr-6 mtime, no fit_policy, no names) because the operator→server scp never happened — the P215 atomic {GMM+checkpoints} rule gated it on a Rung-3 deploy that never came (0/9 promotable).
- **The retrains were NOT invalidated by this**: they consumed the TRAINING-side clean fits via the parquets' regime features (P257's launch verification read the k=6/7/7 vocabulary at startup) — features, GMM, training and validation were one coherent artifact set. The runtime GMM matters only when a checkpoint DEPLOYS to live inference; none did. What the stale runtime fit did contaminate (recorded): live regime labels (the 93%-two-clusters census, constant regime_confidence ~0.99997) and the DRL shadow stream's regime features (P214 caveat).
- **The swap was near-free because the name POOL is shared**: new union = {EXTREME_VOLATILITY, QUIET_ACCUMULATION, MOMENTUM_RALLY, WEAK_CONSOLIDATION, VOLATILE_CHOP, PANIC_SELLOFF, STEADY_UPTREND (ETH/SOL only)}; **NEUTRAL_DRIFT disappears** (its entries in short_bias skip / smart-beta / trend-gate go harmlessly dead) and BTC loses STEADY_UPTREND. Verified every regime-keyed map handles all seven explicitly (kraken_quant `_REGIME_MAP` incl. the P217 additions; smart-beta buckets; ADVISE weights; reversal/dd tables fall to documented defaults). Runtime loader verified k-agnostic; both regime_proba consumers zero-pad k<8 exactly like the training parquets.
- **Deliberate ARMINGS this deploy makes real (labels become honest, so dead entries become reachable):** MOMENTUM_RALLY now exists in all three vocabularies — its ADVISE-weights row, reversal thresholds, and (with STEADY_UPTREND) the P253d gambler regime gate become reachable for the first time (gambler still bounded: OPPORTUNITY mode + conf≥0.70 + rate limits + the ±1-contract sleeve discards its sizing). `regime_confidence` SOFTENS from ~0.99997 to ~0.90-0.92 calibrated (BTC mean_confidence 0.918) — regime-CONFIRM/fusion consumers see honestly lower values. Expect the census to break up from QUIET_ACCUMULATION-58%/two-clusters-93%.
- **Evidence-stream discontinuities, recorded so nobody diffs across them:** (a) the DRL SHADOW obs' regime features now come from a different fit than the loaded (old, leaked-era) checkpoints were trained with — shadow-IC readings across 2026-08-15 are not comparable (the stream was already P214-caveated; the DRL class is retired per P258); (b) `trend_regime_shadow.jsonl` regime labels change definition at the swap — the P198 gate's forward accumulation restarts in effect (its gate set effectively narrows to WEAK_CONSOLIDATION since NEUTRAL_DRIFT no longer occurs); (c) the September P166 candidates are UNAFFECTED — the regime books use their own causal SMA200+funding labels, not the GMM.
- **Guard:** `tests/test_p267_gmm_vocabulary_parity.py` (7) — every name the DEPLOY-side artifacts emit must be explicitly in `_REGIME_MAP` (the P217 pin generalized to read the artifact), names must stay inside a KNOWN_REGIME_POOL (a novel name = a deliberate vocabulary change updating pool+tables in one commit), artifact-set integrity (k==names==weights, 12 features, scaler lengths), and **deploy-side fit_policy must be split_aware** — a full-sample fit reaching models/regime_classifier is the P164/P200 leak arriving at the runtime. Artifact half skips loudly in CI (models/ gitignored, P252b pattern); the pool-coverage half runs everywhere.
- **Mitigation pattern:** "deployed" is a claim about a LOCATION — a deploy-copy that lands on the operator machine while the log says "deployed" is how a clean artifact sat undeployed for a week while everyone believed otherwise. State which host a deploy reached, and give artifact/consumer agreement a guard that reads the ARTIFACT, not a hand-typed list.

### P266. [BUILT + VALIDATED LIVE 2026-08-14] The data-collection remaining-tasks batch — the CoinGlass archive now GROWS, the September scorer sees dailies, a rebuild is ONE step, and the Makefile landmine is defused
Operator asked for the data-collection process to be checked, then for all remaining tasks. Four gaps, each validated by running the real thing, tests in `tests/test_p266_data_collection.py` (17, falsification-probed red):
- **1. `fetch_coinglass_history.py` MERGES (was: bare overwrite of a 180-day rolling window).** The CoinGlass API serves only ~180 days of liquidation/OI depth (measured: 1080 4h rows whatever lookback is requested), and the fetcher overwrote its output parquet — so the trainable history of `liq_imbalance` (the external group's strict-window carrier, P256, and the basis derivflow is forward-testing) was capped at 180 rolling days FOREVER, and a re-fetch after a >180d gap would have permanently lost the middle. Now: union-by-timestamp merge (new wins on collision), atomic write, unreadable-archive kept as `.bak`. **Validated live: 1080 existing + fetch → 1119 rows, coverage 2026-02-08 → 2026-08-14 — the archive now reaches a week PAST the API's own window start (~02-15), the first proof of the growth property.** CADENCE RULE in the script header: re-run at least every ~150 days; `make check` now reports every archive's coverage age (`scripts/check_data_freshness.py` — coverage-end, not file mtime: mtime says when we fetched, coverage says what we have). **[P287: the growth property covered the WRONG FILES — the fetcher's hardcoded `INTERVAL="4h"` grew archives the rebuild never reads, while the consumed `*_1d` files were a one-shot window no command could extend and `make check` watched the 4h names. Fixed 2026-08-16: `--interval`, `refresh-data` runs both, freshness watches the 1d files.]**
- **2. `refresh_ohlcv_4h.py` extends past the monthly boundary via Binance DAILY vision archives (T+1)** — the P264-recorded September logistics item, built. The scorer's price series was bounded by the MONTHLY archives (up to ~31d behind), so the ~09-07..09 P166 forward reads — the September exams — could only join through ~Aug-31. The TRAINING parquet is deliberately never touched (P199's separation stands). **Validated live: coverage 07-31 → 08-13 20:00 (0.5d behind now), and the overlap parity property held exactly — 13,095/13,095 bars vs the training parquet at 0.0 diff.**
- **3. The P253c standing rule is CLOSED: a parquet rebuild is ONE step again.** `build_flow_features.main()` now runs INSIDE `rebuild_pipeline.py` as STEP 5b, after all three assets' parquets are written (cross-asset features need all three), and an fv2 failure FAILS the rebuild (exit 2) — a parquet without fv2 columns looks complete, which is exactly how the P253b rebuild silently dropped all 13 columns. Standalone invocation still works. **Validated live: full `--skip-gmm` rebuild ran end-to-end, 13 fv2 features at 100% coverage on all three assets, all checks + the P252b parity/causality suites green.**
- **4. `make collect` defused; `make refresh-data` is the canonical sequence.** The `collect` target invoked the legacy multi-source `collect_data.py`, which wrote `{ASSET}_60m.parquet` into the SAME directory as the canonical raw parquets — its Kraken source caps at ~720 bars, so one habitual run would have truncated SIX YEARS of canonical training data to ~a month (the P186 phantom/landmine target class, loaded rather than broken). Target is now a loud refusal; the script moved to `archive/legacy_scripts/`. New `refresh-data` target chains the full dependency order (`fetch_binance_full → fetch_binance_funding → fetch_coinglass_history → rebuild_pipeline --smooth 2 --skip-gmm → generate_split_manifest → refresh_ohlcv_4h`) with `--skip-gmm` deliberate and documented in-target: {GMM, parquets, checkpoints} move as ONE versioned set (P215/P253b); a refit is a recorded decision, never a side effect.
- **Still calendar-bound (no code owed):** the August RAW refresh happens when Binance publishes the monthly archive (~Sep 1-2) — one `make refresh-data`. The `_1d` CoinGlass files (oi/liquidation) refresh under the same merge logic whenever a 1d-interval run is done.
- **Mitigation patterns:** (a) an archive fed from a bounded-depth API is the ONLY store of that history — its fetcher must merge, never overwrite, and its staleness must be surfaced where the operator already looks (`make check`), because the loss mode is silent and permanent; (b) freshness is a property of COVERAGE, not file mtime; (c) when a standing rule documents a two-step footgun (P253c), the durable fix is making it one step, not remembering harder.
