### P264. [FIXED 2026-08-11] The ledger inspection found the September gate could not READ the September roster — the regimebook ledgers were registered but unparseable, fixed 28 days before the read
Operator asked whether the four forward ledgers are working properly. Content inspection (server ledgers pulled + inspected + the scorer run END-TO-END on today's rows — the strongest "working" proof):
- **The bug:** `compute_shadow_ic.load_shadow_ledgers` parsed `ts` with `.replace(...)` — ISO-strings only. The P248 regimebook family writes `ts` as an EPOCH FLOAT (`time.time()`, with a separate `iso` field), so the parser raised AttributeError, the per-file handler swallowed it as `failed to read <file>`, and **the raw AND adjusted book ledgers were entirely invisible to the promotion gate** — six strategy-asset rows that would have arrived at the ~09-09 read with zero scoreable records. P248 pinned prefix REGISTRATION at both sites; nothing pinned PARSEABILITY — registered-but-unreadable, the exact P199 class one layer down. The evidence itself was never at risk (the files are fine; a fixed reader re-reads them) — but the read would have failed on its day. Fixed: the parser accepts both shapes; pinned by `test_load_shadow_ledgers_accepts_epoch_float_ts` (red-on-revert by construction).
- **Everything else is HEALTHY, with honest explanations for the quiet parts:** raw books accruing on cadence (no >5h gaps, last rows <1h old), BTC's funding legs LIVE and firing (5 funding_short rows at z>1, currently flat at z=−0.18, `v1_full`); ETH all-flat = trend-only in a peace regime (correct book behavior, not degeneracy); SOL `v1_degraded_no_bear_leg` flat (by design); ADJUSTED books 0/11 rows differing from raw so far — CORRECT, not dead: BTC's params (k_flip=2) only alter FLIPS and none has occurred yet; DERIVFLOW rich and healthy (220 rows, both strategies, full direction spectrum, conf sane); MA_FILTER all-zero for 2 days = the sleeve book has been flat (nothing to filter — honest); BANDED absent (withdrawn, correct); zero zero-confidence-directional rows anywhere (the P236 scorer trap absent).
- **September logistics note (recorded, not yet built):** the local OHLCV refresh uses Binance MONTHLY archives (~31d lag, P199), so the ~09-09 read will join only through ~Aug-31 prices until the September archive lands (~early Oct) — either accept the ~20-day partial first read (n≈120/asset, sufficient for a first verdict) or extend `refresh_ohlcv_4h.py` to the DAILY vision archives (T+1) for a full-window read on the day.
- **Mitigation pattern:** a ledger is "working" only when the tool that will judge it can read TODAY's rows end-to-end — registration, accrual and content-sanity are each necessary and jointly insufficient. Run the consumer against live producer output the day the producer ships, not the day the verdict is due.

### P263. [MEASURED 2026-08-11] The fully-learned retrain — every campaign lesson built in structurally, killed by the CHEAPEST gate in minutes, spending nothing
Operator: "combine what you learned and start retrain." The design (`training/pooled_banded_lab.py`) embodied every lesson AT ONCE: pooled ridge across the three home assets (~3× samples for ~11 params, the parameters-vs-samples lesson), vol-scaled 16h target (the recurring dev winner), banded expression via the single-source `banded_step`, OVERLAY form (P259: standalone fails eras), 250-bar refit (track the current era, P259b), live-computable features from birth — and the P259b ORDERING lesson made structural: a five-gate ladder (design-select → pre-design → **virgin era** → **cross-asset transfer** → only then ONE ledgered validation read → only then forward), where any failure stops the ladder and is reported.
- **Result: DEAD AT STAGE 1.** Best band config's pooled overlay increment = **−1.18 summed** across BTC/ETH/SOL in the DESIGN era itself — the pooled/short-refit variant cannot even help where candidates are easiest to flatter. Compare P259's per-asset variant (won design + pre-design, lost validation): pooling across assets whose signals differ and halving the refit window SUBTRACTED. The close-feature forecast basis is now measured dead through two independent variants and every expression form tried.
- **What this closes:** with TQC (0/9 ×3), the supervised zoo (0/18), meta-labeling (0/12), the banded family (both variants), and now the pooled-learned design all dead, **the forecast-layer question on the current feature basis is exhausted** — not by fiat but by the fastest sequence of honest kills the pipeline has produced. The books ARE the models this data supports; the one live path to a forecast layer is NEW INFORMATION (the liq_imbalance basis derivflow is forward-testing) or the September reads changing the picture.
- **The meta-result worth keeping:** the retrain cost MINUTES and spent NO scarce windows, because every lesson was structural — the ladder's cheapest gate did its job. Contrast the campaign's first retrain (weeks of GPU on a leaked pipeline, graded by a leaked answer key). That delta — from expensive self-deception to instant honest kills — is what the entire P164→P262 arc actually bought.
- **[P263b — the stage-1 death ABLATED (operator: "resolve the dead at stage 1"): the killer is the VOL-SCALED TARGET, and the lesson is that winning ingredients are contextual.** One-factor walk from the dead config to the P259 reference (`training/scripts/p263_ablation.py`, design-era increments, diagnosis-only): dead A −1.18; +refit500 B −1.43 (not the refit); **+raw-target C +0.42 — flipping vol-scaled→raw ALONE rescues it**; +per-asset D −1.48 (not the pooling per se); P259 reference E +1.15 (reproduced); pooled+raw F +0.58. Attribution: **vol-scaling the target — imported as "the recurring dev winner" from the supervised campaigns — is actively destructive under BANDED expression**: the band already normalizes by forecast dispersion, so a vol-scaled forecast is double-normalized and the magnitude information that must clear ABSOLUTE costs is stripped — positions migrate toward low-vol bars where raw edge is smallest. Vol-scaling won at per-bar forecast SCORING; it loses at band-gated EXPRESSION. Secondary: pooling costs ~half the increment (F +0.58 vs E +1.15) — per-asset signal heterogeneity (measured in data_pattern_diagnostics) outweighs the sample-count gain. **Resolution verdict: the death is fully attributed, and no NEW candidate emerges** — every stage-1-passing variant collapses back to the P259 family, whose validation-era verdict is already known-negative (P259b); iterating further against these gates would be selection-by-iteration. The forecast basis stays dead; the durable yield is the design rule: *an ingredient's track record only transfers with its CONTEXT — re-validate every "proven winner" when the expression mechanism changes.*]**
- **[P263c — the deep debug (operator: "continue to debug it"): all three asserted mechanisms verified, and the IC-by-era discriminator shows the family dies for TWO DIFFERENT terminal reasons.** (`training/scripts/p263_debug2.py`; validation-era stats ledgered as diagnosis of the spent P259b read.) **(1) Double-normalization: confirmed, modest** — the vol-scaled variant shifts active bars toward low-vol terciles (SOL hi-vol occupancy 0.453→0.384, lo 0.276→0.326) and takes ~25% fewer positions. **(2) Pooling heterogeneity: confirmed decisively** — five of ten features including the CORE trend features (`mom_540`, `dist_sma200`, `ret_72`, `vol_20`, `funding_z`) carry OPPOSITE-SIGNED ridge coefficients across assets, so pooling averages the loadings toward zero (consistent with data_pattern_diagnostics' per-regime IC sign flips). **(3) The discriminator — forecast Spearman IC by era:** ETH design 0.042 → validation **−0.016** (the signal itself DIED — and the flat-cell decomposition shows ETH's entire design-era increment was 2022-23 BEAR SHORTS, +0.91 gross, which became −0.04 at validation: an era-local signal, unsalvageable by any expression); BTC design 0.017 → validation **+0.032** (the signal HELD — yet the banded entries still lost gross −0.088 long/+0.036 short, because IC ~0.03 is an order of magnitude below the ~0.13 monetization bar: too weak to time entries even through a cost-minimizing band). **Verdict: era-local signal on ETH, sub-monetizable signal on BTC — both terminal, by different mechanisms. No engineering of this family closes a 0.03-vs-0.13 gap; the debug is CLOSED with the corpse fully autopsied.** The one flicker: SOL's validation flat-cell entries were gross-positive both sides (+0.41/+0.42) — consistent with SOL being the policy-layer asset (P241) — but SOL's book already stood over its overlay in-design, and a SOL-only revival would be its own ladder from scratch.]**



### P262. [MEASURED 2026-08-11] The unread-era probe — the P260 caveat attacked with data no selection ever touched, and it splits the roster into CERTIFIED-BEYOND-THE-WINDOW vs STILL-SEPTEMBER
(Committed as `e18a6c4` titled "P261" — renumbered here: a parallel session claimed P261 for the phantom-kill-switch incident minutes earlier, the fourth number collision of the campaign.) The P260 caveat (everything rests on a 7×-read window) was not fully time-bound: three whole years of BTC/ETH (2017-11→2020-08-09, before the parquet start) and five entire assets (BNB/XRP/LTC/DOGE/ADA) were NEVER part of any selection, read, or tuning. `training/unread_era_probe.py` (Binance Vision archives — api.binance.com is geo-blocked 451 from this machine; hard guard: Part A scores no bar on/after the parquet start) sat the surviving mechanisms on all of it:
- **A. VIRGIN ERA (2017-11→2020-08):** ETH trend-only **+186% vs B&H −15%** (positive EVERY year incl. 2018: +20% vs −82%); BTC trend-only **+91% vs B&H +40%** (2018 bear: −28% vs −72%). The trend/hold mechanism's signature (capture trend, dodge crashes) reproduces on data that predates every decision this repo ever made. **The BTC funding legs do NOT confirm there:** active only ~9 usable months (funding history starts late-2019 + 30d z warmup) and they SUBTRACTED in 2020 (book −30% vs trend-only +9%) — thin evidence, wrong direction.
- **B. NEVER-FITTED ASSETS (2020-2026, 10bps RT):** trend-only positive after costs on **5/5** (BNB +406%, DOGE +672%, XRP +276%, ADA +391%, LTC +68%); beats B&H on 1/5 (LTC) — the expected shape for a long/flat filter in monster-bull alts (it trades upside for crash protection). Beats-flat 5/5 on never-touched assets is cross-asset transfer evidence the home window cannot fake.
- **C. DEFLATED SHARPE (Bailey–LdP, from the book's actual validation bar returns):** BTC book SR_ann 1.20 → **DSR 0.48 at N=12 trials, 0.25 at N=50** — the probability the validation Sharpe is real, after the multi-family search, is a coin flip at best. The P260 caveat, now a number.
- **VERDICT — the caveat is PARTIALLY RESOLVED, with a clean split:** the **trend/hold mechanisms (ETH's entire book, SOL's book, BTC's bull leg) are now certified on genuinely unread data** — two orthogonal out-of-selection axes agree, and their September reads become confirmation rather than first evidence. The **BTC funding legs — the entire excess of +33.6% over trend-only's +16.6% — remain uncertified**: virgin-era evidence is thin-and-negative, DSR says the window cannot vouch for them, and P244 already measured funding-cell era-instability. The funding-leg increment (and everything model-shaped) is what ~09-09 actually decides. Artifacts: `training/reports/unread_era_probe_p261.json`.
- **Mitigation pattern:** "all our data is spent" deserves the same skepticism as any other claim — data BEFORE the sample and ASSETS outside the universe are unread windows hiding in plain sight, and a mechanism that transfers to both is certified in a way no amount of re-reading the home window can provide.

### P263. [FIXED 2026-08-10] The sleeve now exists from tick 1 — startup construction closes the P261 window at the source and ends the "no result yet" heartbeat
- **Why (operator-prompted, "why no result yet this process"):** the sleeve was constructed lazily INSIDE the per-tick driver block, which runs AFTER the heartbeat (P227 ordering). Every fresh process therefore ran its first tick with (a) no sleeve equity — the P261 cold-boot window that fired the phantom kill switch twice — and (b) a heartbeat sleeve field with nothing to show, whose "no result yet" text misread as a fault to two different readers in two days (P229, then the operator on 2026-08-10, a day with four restarts).
- **Fix:** construction extracted to `_ensure_coinbase_sleeve()` — **one ctor site** (the P239 config wiring cannot fork, P172; pinned by an exactly-one-`CoinbaseSleeve(`-call test). `run_live` calls it at STARTUP with one `snapshot()` (reconcile + equity) before the first tick, so the P0 feed folds REAL combined equity from tick 1 and the P261 held-combined fallback becomes a backstop instead of the primary path. The driver keeps a retry call for venue-down-at-boot. Fail-soft on every branch (behaviorally tested): any failure degrades to exactly the pre-P263 behavior.
- **Heartbeat:** the sleeve field now leads with the RECONCILED book (venue facts, no "prev tick" hedge needed) and labels the manage result separately: `pos BTC=+1ct; last manage: ... (prev tick)` / `manage pending (driver runs after this message)`. The "no result yet" text is gone and pinned gone.
- Tests: `tests/test_sleeve_startup_p263.py` (11) — fail-soft truth table on a bound fake self, single-ctor-site pin, startup-precedes-loop ordering, snapshot-on-startup (removal falsified → red), heartbeat text. `silent.tryexcept_count` 666→668 hand-bumped (both new guards log; neither startup reconcile nor heartbeat composition may stop the engine, P227).

### P261. [FIXED 2026-08-10 + KILL SWITCH RESET] The phantom −$3,776.44 kill switch — a restart's first tick fed the P0 controller a partial book against combined-denominated persisted anchors
- **The incident (2026-08-11 00:09:19 UTC, operator-reported from Discord):** `[KILL-SWITCH] max_daily_loss, Drawdown=34.76%, Daily P&L=-3776.44` — on a book that was FLAT and healthy. The "daily loss" is the sleeve's entire equity to the cent: the persisted P253 daily anchor ($10,865.13, combined) minus Kraken-only equity ($7,088.69). No real dollar moved; a ±1-nano-contract book cannot lose $3.7k in a day.
- **Mechanism — three individually-correct pieces interacting:** (a) P253 correctly PERSISTED the controller peak and daily anchor (so restarts don't forgive losses) at combined denomination; (b) the sleeve object is built lazily by run_live's heartbeat AFTER the first tick, so a fresh process's first `pre_tick_update` has no sleeve equity; (c) the P227 fold-in only applied its held-combined fallback when the sleeve OBJECT existed, and its closing comment certified the no-object case as safe ("the peak has not seen sleeve equity") — true when written, **falsified the day P253 made the anchors persistent**. Every restart would have re-fired this until fixed. Note the reverse hazard the fix also closes: re-anchoring on the partial equity would produce a phantom +$3,776 GAIN masking real losses all day.
- **Fix:** pure `combined_p0_equity(kraken, sleeve, held)` — fold when known; feed the HELD combined when unknown (unreadable OR not-built-yet); Kraken-only ONLY on a genuinely first-ever boot (where no combined anchor can exist). `_p0_last_combined_equity` is now persisted in the `_save_paper_positions` payload and restored in the governor section via pure `restore_p0_combined_equity()` — the P253 anchors and the value that makes them safe now survive the same restart together. The P253 daily anchor sets from the RESOLVED equity (pinned).
- **Kill switch RESET as part of this incident** (recorded manual reset, confirmation semantics `RESET-20260811`): the activation was arithmetic fiction; leaving it tripped blocks the standing keep-trading decision and trains alert-blindness (P202). Reset by state-file surgery on the volume (`kill_switch_active` false, `risk_state` REDUCED — the same conservative re-entry state `reset_kill_switch()` uses) with the engine stopped.
- **[P261b — the first fix RE-FIRED the kill 21 seconds after the reset, and the reason is the entry's own reasoning:** "no held value = first-ever boot = no anchors exist" is false exactly ONCE — the first boot after deploying this code onto a state file that already carries the combined anchors but predates the new key. Observed live 00:29:20, same −$3,776.44. Fix: `restore_p0_combined_equity` seeds the held value from the persisted **daily anchor** when the explicit key is absent — it is combined-denominated by construction and is the exact number the daily-loss comparison uses, so the first tick's daily P&L reads 0. **Operational close-out (executed 00:38–00:42 UTC by the sibling session, timing correction to this entry's pre-claimed verification):** engine stopped → second surgery (kill False, state REDUCED, verified before/after) → deploy of `eb643a7` (CI green first) → live verification: `[P261] Restored last combined equity $10,865.13`, all three first ticks fed the HELD combined ($10,865.13, not Kraken-only $7,088.69), **zero kill activations**, P0_SAFETY PASS on every chain. **A migration case is a first-boot case with pre-existing state — every "this cannot happen on first boot" argument must be re-checked against deploying onto yesterday's files.**]**
- Tests: `tests/test_p0_cold_boot_equity_p261.py` (9) — the incident's exact numbers as the truth table, first-boot semantics, both persistence halves with the restore assignment flowing THROUGH the pure function. **The first falsification probe (`False and` on the inline restore) passed the textual pins — third catch of that shape in two days (P234, P251, here); the P251 assignment-through-pure-function rule is now applied on sight.**
- **Mitigation pattern:** a comment that certifies a code path as safe ("no spurious drawdown is possible") is a CLAIM whose premises can be silently invalidated by a later change elsewhere — here, persistence added to the anchors it reasoned about. When making state durable, grep for every comment that assumed it was ephemeral.

### P260. [REVIEWED 2026-08-10] Fresh-mind adversarial review of the P253–P259b batch — verdicts rendered, six findings, all addressed same-day
Operator ordered the P247-style fresh-mind review of the whole batch, with two questions. The reviewer verified at source (not from prose) and its findings were then fixed:
- **Q1 (does this round fix the prior rounds' issues): per-issue table, all load-bearing items FIXED at source and code-verified** — funding leak (shift(1) at the rebuild), wavelet causal call, GMM pairing, venue costs (3bps verified in the fold_1 logs to the dollar), lab/live parity by single-source + pins, shadow-IC overlap t. Two honest PARTIALs: circular-selection is closed by PROTOCOL (design-only asserts + the window ledger records overuse rather than preventing it — the validation window now has 7 logged reads), and era-fragility detection works but P259's ordering (ledger wired before the last unread window was read) was an error the batch records rather than structurally prevents. The churn pathology is MEASURED-CLOSED (TQC retired), not repaired.
- **Q2 (can the pipeline produce revenue-generating models): PLAUSIBLE-PENDING-FORWARD-EVIDENCE, not SUPPORTED** — the reviewer's statement of the strongest weakness is now the campaign's official caveat: **every surviving backtest number rests on a 7×-read validation window, and P259b just demonstrated on that exact window that a candidate can win both older eras + the entire same-day battery and still lose there.** The BTC book's +33.6% is a survivor of the same multi-family search on the same window (t≈1.1 before multiplicity). Secondary: the strict-LOO `liq_imbalance` attribution (t≈5.5) rides ~180d of CoinGlass coverage inside a 2.7y window — the probe's t vastly overstates its independent sample; **the derivflow forward read carries more weight than the probe implies.** "Revenue-generating" is a hypothesis with a good protocol; the ~09-09 P166 reads are the first real exam.
- **Six defects found in the batch itself, all addressed:** (1) **`min_hold` does not do what its docstring said** — under continuous exit demand `held` never accrues and a plain exit is blocked INDEFINITELY (escape via flip only); lab and live share the behavior (parity intact, ledger honest), so the MECHANISM stays as-measured and the DOCSTRING was corrected + the true semantics pinned (`TestAdjustSemantics`, 3 tests) + any enforce decision on a min_hold config must first re-lab the intended semantics. (2) **The P259b withdrawal numbers existed in no artifact** — now preserved: `training/scripts/banded_validation_read.py` reproduces the read byte-for-byte (−0.2119/−0.6179 confirmed) and writes `training/reports/banded_validation_read_p259b.json`; deliberately does NOT re-ledger the spend. (3) the rebuild NaN-fill comment now states the bfill head-exception exactly. (4) `pnl_after_cost`'s window-edge conventions documented as deliberate (≤1 trade/window, identical for all candidates). (5) `banded_step`'s vestigial branch annotated (not restructured — byte-behavior is what the recorded reports measured). (6) the seat's 0.9 confidence flagged as a P231-class asserted constant, tolerable only while default-off.
- **Reviewer could not verify (recorded honestly):** the suite's pass status from its interpreter, the post-refactor "identical to 4 decimals" re-run, in-container deploy checks. All three are verified elsewhere in this session's records; noted so the review's scope is exact.
- **Mitigation pattern:** the fresh-mind review paid for itself again (P247's lesson institutionalized) — and its most valuable outputs were not bugs but STATEMENTS: the 7-read window caveat and the liq-coverage caveat are now attached to the numbers they discount, which is the only way survivors of a long campaign stay honestly priced.

### P259. [BUILT + MEASURED + WIRED 2026-08-10] The banded-forecast overlay — the first MODEL family designed around the three measured failure mechanisms, and it earns on BTC and ETH
Operator spec: "models that address all these issues" (the P258 analysis's three mechanisms). The design answers each one BY CONSTRUCTION: (1) information ceiling → the forecast is expressed ONLY through an asymmetric band (enter >t_enter·σ, hold to t_exit·σ) so costs are paid only when the signal clears them; (2) parameters vs samples → ridge on 10 close+funding features (~11 parameters), walk-forward refit; (3) regime-conditionality → optional regime gate + the OVERLAY form (the P250 book keeps every cell where it has an opinion; the model fills only the cells the book leaves FLAT, so the book's edge is never diluted). Plus the constraint the campaign taught: **features restricted to what the live harness computes from its own inputs** (self-fetched closes + persisted funding z) — live parity BY CONSTRUCTION, the inverse of what killed the SOL bear ridge.
- **Lab results (`training/banded_forecast_lab.py`, design era selection, pre-design era stability, NO validation reads — that window is ~spent, survivors go straight to forward):**
  | asset | book design/pre | overlay design/pre | verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | BTC | +1.070 / +0.686 | **+1.217 / +0.744** | **EARNS BOTH ERAS** |
  | ETH | +0.088 / +1.473 | **+1.155 / +1.848** | **EARNS BOTH ERAS** |
  | SOL | +1.210 / +3.302 | +1.180 / +3.954 | book stands (design rule) |
  The first model-family candidate in the campaign to clear the ladder rule AND the era probe. Standalone (non-overlay) banded fails BTC's era check (pre −0.10) — the overlay form is load-bearing, not decoration.
- **Single-source discipline instead of parity tests where possible:** `banded_step` lives in `defense/regime_book_shadow` and the LAB imports it — lab/live mechanism drift is structurally impossible (P172 one-resolver); re-ran the lab post-refactor: identical to 4 decimals. The FEATURE builder needs two implementations (pandas lab / stdlib live) and carries a parity test (≤1e-6 per feature). One deliberate divergence recorded in the export: live normalizes by the CONSTANT exported σ (refreshed per weekly re-export) vs the lab's rolling σ.
- **Wired as the third forward ledger:** `training/scripts/export_banded_models.py` writes `configs/regimebook/{BTC,ETH}_banded.json` (tracked; coefficients + band + provenance; **SOL deliberately NOT exported** — its book stood, pinned by test); the harness loads them and records `regimebook_banded_{asset}.jsonl` (strategy `regimebook_banded`, same glob, zero scorer changes). Its 30d P166 clock starts at the next deploy, landing ~09-09 beside the raw book, the adjusted book, derivflow and ma_filter — **five candidates now sit the same September exam.**
- Tests: +4 in `tests/test_regimebook_seat_p256.py` (41 with the P248 suite): single-source pin, feature parity, overlay semantics (book wins non-flat cells), SOL-absent-by-decision.
- **Same-day KILL BATTERY (operator: "how do we know without waiting") — run to exhaustion, nothing died:** ETH beats the book in **9/9** perturbed band configs, survives **cost×2** both eras (+0.97 vs +0.03 design), wins **all 3** design-era thirds; BTC 7/9 neighbors, survives cost×2 both eras, 2/3 thirds. **But the paired block-bootstrap on the after-cost increment has 90% CIs including ZERO in every era** (ETH design +1.07 [−0.11,+2.20]; BTC +0.15 [−0.19,+0.50]) — at ~6,000 bars an increment of this size is mathematically uncertifiable from history, the campaign's recurring finding. Same-day tests can FALSIFY, never certify; the forward window is not a waiting period, it is where the missing independent samples come from — and it costs nothing (shadow, parallel, zero capital). The one accelerant that exists: read the forward increment's trajectory weekly rather than only at day 30.
- **Mitigation pattern:** when a model class keeps failing, the fix is rarely a bigger model — design the NEXT candidate around the measured failure mechanisms and impose the deployment constraints (live-computable features, shared step functions) at BIRTH, not at porting time. The porting step is where every previous candidate died or drifted.

### P259b. [WITHDRAWN 2026-08-10, same day] The banded overlay FAILED its validation read — the operator's push for same-day knowledge found the era-fragility the entire kill battery could not see
- Operator: "without the shadow trading, we still can know the model performance" — which surfaced the fact that the banded overlay had NEVER taken its single allowed validation-era shot (the P259 protocol had deliberately skipped it as "window ~spent"). The operator-authorized read (ledgered: `banded_overlay_p259` in `training/reports/window_usage.json`, the window's 7th spend) is decisive:
  | asset | overlay val | book val | **increment** | B&H |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | BTC | +0.476 | +0.688 | **−0.212** | +0.024 |
  | ETH | −0.118 | +0.500 | **−0.618** | −0.218 |
  The banded additions LOSE on the one era the selection never touched (late-2024→mid-2026) — on both assets — after winning BOTH older eras and surviving the FULL same-day battery (9/9 perturbed configs, cost×2, all sub-periods). Era-fragility, the exact P243/P244 class. Caveat noted for fairness: the book baseline is itself partially fitted to this window (6 prior reads), but the increment's sign does not depend on that comparison — the banded entries themselves lost money there.
- **Withdrawn immediately:** exports DELETED (`configs/regimebook/{BTC,ETH}_banded.json` — never deployed; the auto-deploy watcher was stopped before shipping them), `export_banded_models.py` gained a `--force-withdrawn` refusal gate citing the read (the P253 SOL-ridge pattern), and the absent-by-decision test now covers all three assets. The lab, the shared `banded_step`, the feature builders and their parity tests are KEPT — the mechanism knowledge is real; the fitted signal is not.
- **Also proven same-day, before the read:** the 10 features pass the P164 construction test (violent future perturbation → past bit-identical; zero leaking features).
- **The epistemology lesson, stated plainly because the whole day argued it:** the same-day kill battery (perturbation, cost-doubling, sub-era splits, bootstrap) CANNOT detect era-conditionality — every one of its checks lives inside the eras the candidate was selected on. Only unread data can. The validation read was the last unread historical window for this candidate; had it passed, the forward ledger remained the only exam left. **A candidate that passes every robustness check you can construct from its own training history has passed nothing about the future** — and the cheap, correct order is: spend the last unread historical window BEFORE wiring a forward ledger, not after (P259 wired first and read second; the right order is the reverse — one day's ledger noise, but the principle stands).
- **Where this leaves the roster:** four candidates sit the September forward exams (raw book, adjusted book, derivflow, ma_filter). The banded family returns only with a design that is era-stable INCLUDING the recent era — e.g., refit windows short enough to track the funding-inversion regime, or the liq_imbalance basis derivflow is already testing.

### P258. [MEASURED + KILLED 2026-08-10] The clean-data control experiment is done: fold_1 failed identically on all three assets — the RL formulation's verdict was never a data artifact, and the round was killed at the decision point it was designed to have
- **The p256_clean TQC run (P257) reached its fold_1 verdicts on all three assets and was killed there** (operator-framed: "check those results and see if we need to kill this round"). The table, all on the leak-corrected parquets at 3bps Coinbase taker:
  | asset | TQC after-cost | trades | cost | B&H | SMA200 | ridge_16h |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | BTC | **−$39,702** (sh −1.57) | 483 | $38,969 | −$49,864 | −$16,782 | **+$409** |
  | ETH | **−$51,757** (sh −0.81) | 482 | $58,329 | −$68,100 | **−$17,905** | −$71,948 |
  | SOL | **−$16,128** (sh −0.40) | 478 | $58,929 | −$91,697 | −$28,389 | **+$6,160** |
  PROMOTION: FAIL ×3. Same signature as both prior campaigns (official_p221b fold_1: −$42.3K/−$61.6K/+$6.1K — SOL is WORSE on clean data), and the diagnostic constant reproduces exactly: **~480 trades/fold at di=4 = the policy changes position at ~97% of decision points** (P242's churn measurement, unchanged by clean inputs).
- **What this round uniquely answered, and why fold_1 suffices:** it was the CONTROL EXPERIMENT for "were the 0/9×2 failures dirty-data artifacts?" — the first TQC training with clean inputs end-to-end. Answer: **no** — the failure is structural to the formulation (no forecast edge at the horizon + churn pathology under real costs), exactly as the P254 probe predicted (clean-parquet signal landscape ~unchanged). Folds 2–3 would re-measure a twice-known cross-fold shape at ~12–20 GPU-hours; killed per the P221 precedent (kill-after-fold_1, "its result stands"). Fold_1 artifacts + eval logs preserved under `models/retrained/{A}/p256_clean/fold_1/` + `training/reports/p256_clean_logs/`.
- **The same day's full DS lifecycle (p257_full: feature lab → EDA → engineered ladder → assembly) REPRODUCED the P250 verdicts to the decimal on the rebuilt parquets** (BTC perp +33.61%/no flags; ETH perp +1.06%; SOL perp −22.91%/ERA; spot books likewise) — triple confirmation: disk == lab-clean data, the lifecycle is deterministic, and the roster survives re-derivation from scratch. Reports: `training/reports/regime_lab_{select,assemble}_p257_full.json`, `regime_lab_eda.json`.
- **The RL question is now CLOSED at the strongest evidence standard this project can produce:** three independent campaigns (two harness-clean, one fully input-clean), 21 fold-level measurements, zero promotable — plus survey-level literature consensus that RL's failure mode under transaction costs is exactly the churn observed. Any future RL work requires a NEW formulation premise (a proven signal basis to exploit, or a policy-layer role on an already-validated book), never another retrain of this design. The candidates that remain alive are the ones already sitting forward exams: the regime books (raw + adjusted), derivflow (the liq_imbalance carrier, P256), and ma_filter — all reading out ~2026-09-07..09.
- **Mitigation pattern:** when a campaign exists to answer ONE question, define the decision point before launch and stop there — fold_1 across three assets answered "clean data?" completely, and the remaining folds had no question left to answer. A run allowed to finish out of momentum is GPU-hours converted into nothing.
